The bot does not budget the rover's 3.5 t against the battery packs. Not tuned.

**What was left or decided differently.**
- Where the two later sections of the design differ, the code follows the REVISION section (`IsRobotLive`, which counts a rover docked at a pad as live; `FindOutpostDestination(.., bIgnoreRoom, ..)`; the hint alert G3; stages 4 and 5): the brief names the MERGED section as authoritative, but the REVISION says it replaces it and the mechanics agree.
- The "final load" minimum is 25 kg, a constant. The design said `HaulLoadMinKg (25 kg)`, but that value is 100 kg in the code (`max(25, PilesCapacity / 5)`), and 100 would strand a 40 to 90 kg remnant.
- The MERGED section's extra stage-4 case (freight open but no rover reaches its pickup by road, the C6 split network) is not built; the REVISION reads that review item as stage 5 and that is what is built.
- The bot orders its rover from the first pylon, not "with the drill", so it rides the next ship and waits at the drill. It opens only the first outpost found (it surveys every hint).
- The G3 alert and the guide name the hint centre, so Ice_C reads "about 690 m east-south-east" for a 720 m site.
- Not run: the line-for-line diff of `RH_PLAYTEST_OUTPOST=0` against a C6c baseline build (it needs a baseline worktree build; the switch only skips `OutpostStep`, so no other bot line can change), and a mid-trip cargo-return check (the harness rover is not a Mass entity; a real rover's cargo is returned by `SaveColony` as in C6).
- The checkpoints were re-made (`rh_checkpoints.sh all --force`: Midgame sol 28.35, Late sol 62.35) and both load (`Colony loaded: slot 'Midgame'` and `'Late'`).

### D2, more shafts: what it settles, and where it deviated (2026-10-21, not yet committed)

Built from `docs/qa/2026-10-21/d2-shafts-design.md` (sections 1-10 as amended by its REVISION, which wins where they differ). Save **v44**, strict (W1 took v43). A list of shafts, each with its own head, depth, bore target, carve queue and anchoring Borer, on shared floors (one lattice per level). With one shaft nothing moves: every battery line is identical except the eight lines that print the save version.

**Deviation accepted 2026-10-21 (the director's option a): reach is floor-wide.** Per-dig reach is deferred to D6.

> **What the plan said.** "A floor's cells are reached when a shaft that reaches that floor touches them" (`going-deep-plan.md:36`). That would make each separate dig its own place: its own air, its own rating, its own reach.
>
> **What D2 builds instead.** A floor is still one place, however many digs it has. Once any shaft reaches floor -1, the whole of floor -1 counts as reached: you can build on any carved cell, it has one air supply, one Livable rating, one district and one commute anchor. Which shaft "touches" a dig only decides which shaft head a robot or a colonist uses. It never decides whether the dig can be used.
>
> **What you will notice:**
> - **A dig with no air filter is rated through another dig's filter.** Air filters count anywhere on the floor (`IsFloorCirculated`), and so does the size minimum (`bBigEnough`). So a two-cell dig under the new shaft is Livable the moment the main dig is.
> - **A new shaft links nearby surface habitats to floor -1's air**, even before it has dug anything on -1. The habitat link is "a bay near any shaft head that has reached floor -1, while floor -1 is rated" (`IsBayNearShaftHead`, used by `IsNetShaftLinked` and the demolish check).
> - **(R2) Commutes between two digs on one floor cost 0 m.** A colonist who lives in one dig and works in the other is "on the same floor", and a same-floor commute is already 0 today. Floor residents have no cell of their own (only `HomeLevel`), so D2 cannot measure that walk.
>
> **The alternative: per-dig reach.** Each 4-connected dig that a shaft touches becomes its own place. It would fix the three effects above, and cost a re-key of every Level-keyed store (`FloorO2Kg`, `RatedFloors`, `FloorsNotedSmall`, `FloorRoomCells`, `FloorClimate`, the districts' `FloorOwner`, the colonist `HomeLevel`, `CarveQueue`, growth targets and queued orders), a merge rule for D3's tunnels, and a change to `deep.log` (D1's painted island would become unreached): two to three sessions with the battery at risk.
>
> **Recommendation (taken).** Keep reach floor-wide in D2. Move per-volume air and reach to D6 (caverns), and give floor homes a cell there so the commute can see digs (D4/D6). The two effects are pinned by `-shafts` (SH11 and SH11b), so changing the rule later is a visible, tested change.

**Two more deviations, logged as the REVISION asks.**
- **Era logistics teleports, so era ignores which shaft serves a site** (as C4 did for roads). The agent band drives to the serving head (`ServingShaft`). The bar is the usual 5% ledger over 10 sols with two shafts (SH12): the Borer ledgers agree (40 + 40 cells in both bands). Era bores and carves every shaft through the same `StepProduction` code, in the same order, and does not refuse while a shaft is being sunk.
- **Shaft 0's column keeps today's behaviour for floor buildings** (O4's known gap). Tests place floor buildings at the head, so refusing (0,0) would move battery lines. A floor building on the column of a shaft k > 0 is refused ("That is a shaft column"), and SinkShaft refuses a column with a building on it.

**What changed.**
- **Sim data.** `FRHShaft` (Id, RegionId, Column, HeadCm, Depth, BoreTargetDepth, CarveQueue, BorerId) replaces `ShaftDepth`, `ShaftHeadCm`, `BoreTargetDepth` and `CarveQueue`. Derived, never saved: `DeepestShaft`, `ShaftColumns`, `BorerShaft`, `ShaftsVersion`, the dig labelling cache. `RH::MaxShaftsCap` = 16 is the one constant behind both the `MaxShafts` config clamp (row 4, a placeholder) and the load refusal.
- **Getters (R6).** `GetShaftDepth()` is the deepest shaft; `GetBoreTargetDepth()` is shaft 0's; `GetShaftHeadCm()` is the origin (shaft 0's head); `GetCarveQueued(L)` is the sum. Each has a per-shaft overload, and the deck, `RH.Status` and the Borer card moved to them in this gate (one deck click with shaft 1 at -3 would otherwise order shaft 0 to -4).
- **The work loop.** A Borer works the shaft it anchors, else shaft 0 as a helper. The bore-in-flight scan counts only its own shaft's records; the carve gate is `ShaftReaches(S, L)`, never "the deepest shaft reaches L"; `ExcavateFloor(L, N, Why, S)` and `PeekCarveCells(L, N, Out, S)` take the shaft (INDEX_NONE = the lowest Id that reaches the floor).
- **Carving.** Plan points belong to the nearest shaft that reaches the floor (ties to the lower Id); each shaft has its own spiral cursor around its column, which skips carved cells and every shaft column; a plan point is stepped over without moving the cursor.
- **Orders.** `SinkShaft` (Value = the Borer's Id), refusals 1-9 at execute time (`CanSinkShaft`, also what the deck pre-checks). `Bore` and `Excavate` pick the shaft by `Cmd.Target` (empty = shaft 0, `Shaft<k>`, anything else refused as unknown).
- **Serving head.** `ServingShaft(L, P)`: the shafts that reach L; of those, the ones that touch P's dig (4-connected, labelled in ordinal order, cached) when any do; the nearest column (int64 squared distance), ties to the lower Id. `GetApproachPoint` uses it. The commute takes the cheapest head per trip, ties to the lower Id, falling back to shaft 0's when none reaches the floor; the memo key stays shaft 0's head, the flows key gains `ShaftReach` (up on a sink, and on a bore only with more than one shaft), so `CommuteDijkstraRuns` and `CommuteFlowRebuilds` cannot move with one shaft.
- **Save v44.** The shaft block sits straight after the floor table (the K6 offsets do not move): count, then per shaft Id, RegionId, Depth, BoreTargetDepth, BorerId, the head (shaft 0) or the column (shaft k), and the queue as sorted (Level, cells) pairs. The old slot keeps the spoil pile and the in-flight work. Load refusals go through `OutError` ("Save shaft list invalid: ...") before teardown: count outside 1..16, ids not 0..n-1, a region other than 0, depth or target out of range, a shaft k with shaft 0 unbored, a repeated column or (0,0), a column that is a cell or a planned cell, a queue key outside range or below its shaft's depth. A BorerId that names no Borer, or a Borer that anchors two shafts, is repaired with a Warning.
- **Presentation (minimal).** Each later shaft gets its own anchor, static cab, fill light and deck plate, shown only on floors that shaft reaches; the pit rig opens every column that reaches the floor and the sand skirt spans every head; the deck's Bore acts on the selected Borer's shaft; the Borer card says which shaft it anchors or helps and gains "Sink a shaft here" (with the reason when it cannot); a "Next shaft" button walks the camera head to head; the excavate drag sends its order to the serving shaft (no order where none reaches); the elevator sound uses the head nearest the camera. Cheats: `RH.Bore <d> [shaft]`, `RH.Excavate <l> <n> [shaft]`, `RH.ExcavateNow <l> <n> [shaft]`, `RH.SinkShaft <BorerId>`, `RH.SinkNow <Xm> <Ym> [Depth]`, one `RH.Status` line per shaft. Wording is placeholder: rows H1-H7 of `docs/qa/2026-10-15/gate-d-wording.md`.

**Deviations from the REVISION (small).**
- `CanSinkShaft` is public so the deck can pre-check the preflight. The harness path `Debug_SinkShaftAt` tests the head (there is no Borer) for refusal 6 and skips refusals 1-3.
- SH13 tests the two calls growth's `CompleteFloorCells` makes (`ExcavateFloor` and `PeekCarveCells` with no shaft named), not a whole growth project.
- The SH12 era / agent pair compares the Borer ledger (cells carved on each shaft), not regolith totals: the suite's base colony carries the standing `Dig Regolith_A` order, which the era band digs and a robot-less agent leg does not.
- Crew still board the elevator at shaft 0 whichever dig they live in, and a later shaft's cab does not slide its doors (D3 and D4 route crew through the head that serves them). The one-shaft before/after capture was not made: the baseline binary is not kept, and the sim identity is the battery's.

**Tests.** New fails-loudly `-shafts` suite, **145 checks**, in `rh_battery.sh` (now 46 suites and 47 runs): SH0 the fresh colony; SH1 one shaft is D1 (bitwise head, serving shaft 0 everywhere, the old approach formula with `==`); SH2 and SH2b the refusals, one per preflight item on both the order and the harness path, and the execute-time race; SH3 the sink; SH4 Bore and Excavate by Target; SH4b the work loop (concurrent bores, the helper never doubles a bore or takes shaft 1's queue, the former helper works only its new shaft, a save and load mid-batch); SH5 two Borers carve 12 cells each on one floor, literal walks, interleaved ordinals; SH6 a column on a cell; SH7 plan ownership and a later shaft taking points over; SH8 and SH8b which head serves (digs, islands, a joined line, rule 2); SH9 haulers and build claims; SH10 commute heads (hand-computed integers, the tie, the road load); SH11 and SH11b the air link and the floor-wide reach, pinned; SH12 save, load and era (a mid-batch save equals a control that never saved, seven hand-made files refused with the colony untouched, the era / agent pair); SH13 growth's calls. Mutation-checked: counting any bore in flight (not only the shaft's own) fails SH4b (two checks), and dropping the "touches the dig" rule fails SH8b.

**Battery.** `bash scripts/sim/rh_battery.sh 10`: BATTERY COMPLETE: 0 suite(s) not green, 47 logs, all newer than `libUnrealEditor-RedHopeSim.dylib`, no file in Source or docs/data newer than `baseline.log`; both pins hold (`power: gen 50 W load 20 W`, `deposit Regolith_A: 40645 kg`). `python3 scripts/sim/diff_battery.py Saved/baselines/battery_post_w1 Saved/RHBattery` shows 16 differing lines and the new `shafts.log`: **8 lines out, 8 in, every one a save version**: `-traffic` X15 (the file says version, the previous-version refusal), `-deep` K6 (the file says version, the hand-made file, the previous-version refusal), `-farice` F16 (the file says version with the refusal, the v-N save loads: W1 added these two) and `-framing` e5. (A first run of the same battery also moved four wall-clock digits: `-arrivals` ARRV e 0.05 to 0.04 ms and `-traffic` X16 2.1 to 2.2 s; the second run did not.)

**-scale.** Outside the battery. Every one-shaft line is unchanged against a W1 baseline built in a worktree (digests `5f9d7352` at N=5000, commute `5c609384` and `1e609e47`, rover plans `a579cc22`, every count; only wall-clock digits differ, all inside noise). The new four-shaft block (3 shafts sunk 600 m out, 3,150 cells carved by them, so 4,201 cells on five floors of four digs, the new cells zoned Living): cold dig labelling 0.08 ms worst of five floors (budget 1), 2,500 `GetApproachPoint` 3.65 ms (budget 5), commute with four heads over 500 anchors cold 5.5 ms (the one-shaft budget is 10; one shaft measures 3.9) and flows only 0.26 ms (budget 1), `GetColonyHope` on five far-dig floors 0.28 ms (budget 0.5; the hash path ran 250 times).

**Playtest and checkpoints.** `RH_PLAYTEST_DISTRICTS=1 bash scripts/sim/rh_playtest.sh 110 d2_full guide full` is byte-identical to `Saved/RHPlaytest/w1_full.txt` (1,437 lines, `cmp` clean). `rh_checkpoints.sh all --force` re-made Midgame (sol 28.35) and Late (sol 62.35), and both load (`Colony loaded: slot 'Midgame'` and `'Late'`). Captures: `Saved/RHCapture/d2/d2_two_shafts_00..02.png` (floor -1 with both digs, the second dig close, the second Borer at the second head).

### Playtest bot: night storage before rovers (2026-10-21, not yet committed)

Bot only (`Source/RedHope/Private/RHPlaytest.cpp`, `scripts/sim/rh_playtest.sh`); no sim change, no data change. It answers the W1 knock-on: the full run ended with food made 61.5 against 71.3 drawn, because the garden's grow lights draw on the bank round the clock and the bank was empty.

- **What the books showed.** The bot's battery packs were only ever the cells' rule (0.2 of a bank a cell, owed when a floor is ordered) and went in last, after six Spare Parts Crates, the soil and seed pairs, the rover (3.5 t), the Luxury Goods and two Solar kits, so a full ship refused them (the sol-96 ship carried 4 of 7 owed). Even the rule's own number is low: 40 lit cells draw 2.4 kW and the machines about 2.3 kW, over 12 sol-hours of dark that is 14 banks of 4,000 Wh, not 8. At HEAD the bank was at or near empty on all but three sols from 5 to 110 in the full run.
- **The rule** (`Cargo3` block of `Tick`, with the other cargo rules; logged as a `LIVING  night storage` line per ship). At each third-or-later ship, with the living city and life support on: the lit cells are the cells planted, those the stores can plant, and this ship's own soil and seeds (`Planted + Fundable + 4 x (1 + pairs)`); the load is the machines' mean load over the last whole sol (new `LastLoadW`, from the bot's own power tally) plus 60 W a lit cell; `need = ceil(load x 12 sol-hours / 4,000 Wh)`, at most 24 banks. The banks standing, on their sites or waiting in the import stock are counted off, the short ones (and never fewer than the cells' rule owes, at most 12 a ship) go into the manifest right after the crates and the pairs, **before the rover**. The rover then rides only if it fits in what the award has left (`ManifestListMassKg`: manifest-row masses, the same sum the sim's budget check uses); if not it waits for the next ship (a `LIVING  the Cargo Rover(s) wait` line, counted in the REPORT). The bookkeeping that pays the owed packs after the load is unchanged.
- **Switch.** `nightbank=0` (`RH_PLAYTEST_NIGHTBANK=0`) returns the previous bot: proved line-identical to HEAD apart from the one new REPORT line (full, lean and 75% runs: 1 differing line each). Without districts, and with `roads=0`, the code is not reached: 0 differing lines against the HEAD binary (50-sol and 110-sol runs).
- **Report.** A new `REPORT  night storage` line: sol-hours the bank stood empty and the grow lights were dark over the run, banks at the end, packs the rule added beyond the cells' rule, rover deferrals.
- **A rule I tried and dropped.** Capping the lit cells at what the food leg needs for the people planned for (the wave's, once it is in sight) changed nothing: the wave is not in sight on the ship before it, and the cells that light by sol 90 are the ones already funded in the stores. Counting the funded cells is what moved the full run.

**Playtests** (110 sols, districts, guide order, 0 sim errors; before is HEAD, `nightbank=0`):

| | full before | full after | lean before | lean after | 75% full before | 75% full after |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| people, recalled | 115, 0 | 115, 0 | 115, 0 | 115, 0 | 118, 0 | 119, 0 |
| Big Arrival sol | 98.01 | 98.01 | 99.01 | 98.01 | 68.01 | 65.01 |
| food made / drawn (kg a sol) | 61.5 / 71.3 | 74.3 / 71.3 | 63.8 / 71.3 | 67.6 / 71.3 | 100.0 / 73.2 | 100.0 / 73.8 |
| water made / drawn | 1,185 / 35 | 900 / 35 | 1,215 / 35 | 885 / 35 | 915 / 35 | 855 / 36 |
| rover freight | 38.8 t | 44.4 t | 49.7 t | 44.2 t | 39.0 t | 40.2 t |
| rovers ordered | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 | 1 |
| bank stood empty, sol-hours of 2,640 | 712 | 465 | 632 | 397 | 560 | 556 |
| sols from 40 with the bank near 0% | 66 | 0 | 68 | 0 | 37 | 22 |
| grow lights dark, sol-hours | 457 | 213 | 247 | 16 | 302 | 302 |
| Battery Banks at the end | 8 | 18 | 10 | 17 | 14 | 17 |
| Hope range | 53-100 | 53-100 | 53-100 | 53-100 | 54-100 | 54-100 |

- W1's bar holds in all six: water made over 0, freight over 0 t, every ship after the first outpost load with Water room for the settlers it housed, 0 recalled. The rule never had to defer the rover (0 deferrals: the awards, 11.6 to 15.4 t, always left room for the 3.5 t after the crates, the pairs and the packs, so the deferral branch ran in no playtest and is checked by reading only), so it spends no ship mass on it; the cost is the Solar kits it displaces at the end of the manifest. The rover is ordered a little earlier (sol 41.59 against 41.62 in full) and carries more freight in the full runs.
- The remaining empty hours are the first 37 sols (before there are banks or lights) and, in the 75% run, sols 67-87: the wave lands at sol 65 and the garden lights 40 cells before the next ship's banks stand. The lights cannot be provisioned for a wave the ship before it cannot see.
- **Food made against drawn at sol 110 passes in the full and 75% runs, and misses in the lean run (67.6 against 71.3).** The bank is not why: from sol 40 the bank never empties in the lean run. The lean garden's last 12 cells are planted at sol 99 (the wave ship) and the measured production, a three-sol window, climbs about 2.5 kg a sol while they come in; the same lean run to sol 125 reads 70.0 / 71.9 at sol 112, 72.0 / 71.9 at 114 and 83.0 / 73.8 at 124, with 6,500 kg (92 sols) in store throughout. Planting that batch earlier means a ship carrying the wave's soil before the wave is in sight (C6c's `GWaveStreakShare`, which was set the other way on evidence), so it is left alone here.

### D3, tunnels: what it settles, and where it deviated (2026-10-21, not yet committed)

Built from `docs/qa/2026-10-21/d3-tunnels-design.md`, in the order of authority the director set: the DIRECTOR ANSWER, then the REVISION (with its option-(c) table), then the PROFESSOR REVIEW, then the original design. **Q-A (c), 2026-10-21: tunnels only.** Save **v45**, strict (D2 took v44). With no tunnel nothing moves: every battery line is identical except the nine lines that print the save version, and the new `tunnels.log`.

**What D3 builds (option c).** A painted line of ordinary carved lattice cells that joins digs on one floor, carved by the Borer.
- **The order.** `DigTunnel` (`Level`, `Location` = A, `LocationB` = B, snapped to the lattice) and `CancelTunnel` (`Value` = the tunnel's Id). The preflight (`CanDigTunnel`, also what the deck's brush pre-checks) runs again at execute time and refuses, each with a reason: a floor no shaft reaches; a chain under 2 or over `TunnelMaxCells` cells; a start that is carved or has no carved 4-neighbour ("start a tunnel at the edge of a dig"); any chain point already carved; any chain point a shaft column; any chain point an uncarved point of another open tunnel; more than `MaxOpenTunnels` open. No bill: the cost is Borer work.
- **The chain** is literal and derived: an L from A along X to (B.X, A.Y), then along Y to B, both ends included (|dx| + |dy| + 1 points). The record (`FRHTunnel`: Id, RegionId, Level, A, B, two-ended, cancelled) is all that is saved. `bTwoEnded` is set when B stands beside a carved cell; then B's end is carved too.
- **Carving.** Each cell is one `CarveCell` batch (3 sol-hours, 500 W or H2, 1,200 kg spoil at the Borer): same recipe, same cell. After bore and the carve queue found nothing, the Borer of shaft S scans open tunnels by Id on floors S reaches and takes the A front if `ServingShaft` of the front's carved neighbour is S, else the B front (two-ended). A tunnel starts a batch only while its uncarved points outnumber its batches in flight. The work record is `(2, Id)` for the A end and `(3, Id)` for the B end, so `PendingBoreWork` keeps its `FIntPoint` format and the bore-in-flight scan (X == 0) is unchanged. At completion the cell is the first uncarved chain point from the recorded end, read then, appended through `AppendCarvedCell`: ordinals stay add-only and every cell grows from a cell carved before it.
- **O9 extended.** SinkShaft refusal 8 gains "an uncarved point of an open tunnel", so a column cannot land mid-chain and strand it. A cancelled tunnel stops protecting its rock once no batch is in flight.
- **The dig merge** needs no code: `EnsureDigInfo` labels 4-connected digs over the floor's cells, so the last chain cell joins two digs into one, and `Touching` then lists both shafts.
- **Cancel.** No new batch starts; the cells carved stay; a batch in flight lands its cell (so a cancel never leaves a half-carved cell).
- **Save v45.** The tunnel block sits straight after the shaft block (K6's offsets and SH12's `BlockAt` do not move): `NextTunnelId`, `TunnelCount`, then per tunnel Id, RegionId, Level, A.X, A.Y, B.X, B.Y, two-ended, cancelled (an int32 each; a bool is four bytes). **The block is 8 bytes with no tunnel, 8 + 36 per tunnel** (TN8 checks 72 bytes for two). Under (c) there is no lift count: no dead saved field, and `FRHRobotSaveState::Level` stays the dead 0 until D4. Load refusals go through `OutError` ("Save tunnel list invalid: ...") before teardown: a count or next Id out of range, ids not ascending or not below `NextTunnelId`, a region other than 0, a floor outside range or reached by no shaft, a chain under 2 or over `RH::TunnelMaxCellsCap` (256), a start neither dug nor beside a dug cell, a two-ended tunnel whose B is neither, a shaft column on an open tunnel's uncarved line, two open tunnels sharing an uncarved point, more than `RH::MaxOpenTunnelsCap` (64) open. A work record naming a tunnel the file no longer lists completes with its spoil and no cell (a Display line), as the O5 live case does.
- **Config.** `TunnelMaxCells` 64 (clamped 2..256) and `MaxOpenTunnels` 8 (clamped 1..64) in `RH_Config.csv` and the live `DT_Config` (synced, 236 -> 238 rows); TN0 reads them back from the DT, so drift fails loudly.
- **Presentation.** `URHTunnelVisualizerSubsystem` (a pure listener, const accessors only): on the floor view, the uncarved part of an open tunnel is a dashed amber ghost, the carved part a pale centre stripe, and each unfinished tunnel a label (cells left / stalled / cancelled). The deck's SHAFT strip gains the **Tunnel** brush (press anchors A, drag aims B with the live cell count, Borer-hours, spoil and the preflight's reason, release transmits; right-click exits) and a **TUNNELS** card listing the viewed floor's open tunnels (cells left, "digging" or "stalled", click to cancel). Console: `RH.DigTunnel`, `RH.CancelTunnel`, `RH.TunnelNow`, `RH.TunnelGhost`, and a line per tunnel in `RH.Status`. Wording: Gate-D rows I1-I9 (`docs/qa/2026-10-15/gate-d-wording.md`).

**Deviations from `going-deep-plan.md`, as the REVISION lists them (1-8).**
1. **Tunnels are carved lattice cells, not road-graph edges with a Level** (`going-deep-plan.md:71`). One built tunnel edge in `RoadGraph` would switch every surface robot onto `MoveAlongRoads` (`HasBuiltRoads()`), and the whole battery with it.
2. **The era refusal while a tunnel is carved is dropped** (`going-deep-plan.md:70`). Carving is `StepProduction` work that `EraStep` runs, like the Excavate carve queue, which `CanEnterEraMode` does not refuse either; the road refusal exists because era parks the Fabricators who build roads, which does not apply to Borer work. A front served only by an unanchored shaft would also have made the lock unbounded. TN7 pins it: the gate answers the same with and without an open tunnel, era finishes the same tunnel in the same cell order as the agent band, a stalled tunnel does not block era, and a cancel stops it.
3. **Era logistics teleport past lifts and tunnels**, as C4 and D2 logged. They are level-matched and ignore robot levels.
4. **Rovers stay on the surface in D3.** The plan's "robots and rovers go down" waits for D4 (trams).
5. **The commute ignores tunnels until floor homes and seats get a cell (D6).** R2 (a 0 m commute between digs on one floor) is deferred. TN18 pins that a carved tunnel moves no commute number.
6. **D2 REVISION O5's promise** ("D3's joined graph replaces both with one route", `d2-shafts-design.md:468`) **is not kept.** Robots and crew may still show different heads.
7. **The plan's D3 bar "a commute across two shafts"** (`going-deep-plan.md:72`) is covered by TN18 plus `-shafts` SH10, not by a new commute.
8. **Under (c) only: robots-below (Q1a) moves from D3 to D4**, with lift capacity. D3's robot design and the REVISION's items 1-4 and 6 carry into D4 unchanged. Capacity in D4 is per plan, not a deviation.

**Built and not built.** Not built, and no stub left for any of it (moved to D4): fitted lifts and `FitLift`, portals, `PlanDeepRoute` and `FRHDeepNet`, robots riding and the level written at a ride's end, the cross-level claims and the one ranking key (items 1 and O9), the charge reserve (item 2), interrupted rides and the load repair of robot levels (item 3), repair across levels (item 4), the `lifts=1` playtest variant (item 6), TN1 and TN8-TN17 of the robot half, and O3, O7, O8. `FRHRobotSaveState::Level` and `CollectRobotStates` are untouched. Adopted and built: O1, O2, O4, O5, O10, O11 (below). Rejected: O6 (shelter from wear below; D5's balance call).

**Optional items, decided.**
- **O1 (civic growth skips tunnel cells): adopted.** `IsCellFreeForRoom` refuses every point of any tunnel's chain; the player may still zone one by hand. Inert with no tunnel. Pinned in TN4.
- **O2 (the head shift at a merge): adopted.** TN5 pins `ServingShaft` (and so the fallback head) for cells of both digs before and after: dig B's cells stay head 1's, dig A's head 0's, and a tunnel cell goes to the nearer head, with the approach point of a site on one.
- **O4 (the batch-start line): adopted.** A tunnel batch logs `Borer #n CarveCell started (3 h, tunnel t, A end, floor L)`; the bore and carve line is untouched.
- **O5 (the live empty completion): adopted.** A completion that finds no uncarved chain point (or a column on it) drops nothing but the cell: its spoil was already dropped with the batch, a Display line says so, and a transient counter (`GetTunnelBatchesEmpty`) lets TN6d assert it with two Borers, one chain point left for two batches and the spiral taking the other.
- **O10 (seeded random cases): adopted** for TN10: 50 rounds from `FRandomStream(0xD3)` of random orders, carves and cancels, asserting the adjacency invariant, no repeats, no column cell, every unfinished tunnel still having a front, and a save and load that digest equal.
- **O11 (show a stalled tunnel): adopted.** `IsTunnelStalled` (open, no batch in flight, no completed Borer serves either front) feeds the floor label and the TUNNELS card.
- **Moved to D4 with the robots (not built):** O3, O7, O8, O9.

**Tests.** New fails-loudly `-tunnels` suite, **152 checks**, 0 failures, in `rh_battery.sh` (now 47 suites and 48 runs): TN0 the fresh colony, the caps and both config rows read back from the live DT; TN1 no tunnel is D2 (the spiral, the empty 8-byte block, growth's free-cell test untouched); TN2 every `DigTunnel` refusal 1-7 by its reason (on the harness path and the order), the ninth open tunnel, `CancelTunnel`'s three refusals, the sink refusal and its release by a cancel, and the execute-time race for both a dug start and a column sunk on the line; TN3 the chain against a one-step-at-a-time walk for six lines, `IsOnTunnelChain` over a grid, an absurd point refused without overflow; TN4 one Borer carves (2,0) to (6,3) in order, 3-sol-hour batches, adjacency, growth skips the cells (O1), the spiral goes on skipping them; TN5 two Borers meet on a 28-cell two-ended tunnel, the digs merge, no cell twice, 28 batches for 28 cells, `ServingShaft` pinned before and after (O2); TN6 a spiral cell and a plan point on the chain (the plan point leaves the plan, so the colony still saves), a shaft sunk mid-batch, and the live empty completion (O5: two batches, one point left); TN7 the era gate unchanged by an open tunnel, a stalled tunnel not blocking era, the same 40 cells in the same order in the agent band and in era over 10 sols, a cancel in era; TN8 a save mid-batch with an open, a finished and a cancelled tunnel equals the control that never saved, the layout read back (72 bytes for two tunnels), thirteen hand-made refusals, a shaft sunk on a cancelled tunnel's rock saves and loads, and a batch whose tunnel the file no longer lists lands with no cell; TN9 stalled; TN10 50 seeded random rounds (20 tunnels ordered, 30 refused for 3 kinds of reason, 6 cancelled, 200 cells); TN18 a carved tunnel moves no commute number. Mutation checks: with the plan removal, the sink refusal and growth's skip each broken in one build, TN2, TN4 and TN6b failed (5 checks) and nothing else did.

**Battery.** `bash scripts/sim/rh_battery.sh 10`: `BATTERY COMPLETE: 0 suite(s) not green`, 48 logs, all newer than `libUnrealEditor-RedHopeSim.dylib`, no file in Source or docs/data newer than `baseline.log`; both pins hold (`power: gen 50 W load 20 W`, `deposit Regolith_A: 40645 kg`). `python3 scripts/sim/diff_battery.py Saved/baselines/battery_post_nb Saved/RHBattery` shows 18 differing lines and the new `tunnels.log` (1,734 sim lines): **9 lines out, 9 in, every one a save version**, the recount the REVISION asked for (the count does not depend on the block's size, so (c) leaves it at 9): `-deep` K6 (the file says version, the hand-made file, the previous version: 3), `-farice` F16 (the version line and the literal "the v44 save loads", which the REVISION adds to D2's list: 2), `-framing` e5 (1), `-shafts` SH12 (1), `-traffic` X15 (2). The four `Contains(TEXT("/44"))` conditions moved to `/45` with their text and their previous-version constants.

Every one-shaft line is unchanged (digests `5f9d7352` at N=5000, commute `5c609384` and `1e609e47`, rover plans `a579cc22`, as at D2). The new tunnel block (eight tunnels, the most MaxOpenTunnels allows, on the five floors of four digs, 360 cells in all): ordering 0.06 ms; 2,000 preflights of full-length lines, each refused only at the open-tunnel cap after the whole chain was walked, 1.5 ms (budget 100); 10,000 uncarved plus front reads and 2,000 stalled reads 28.8 ms (budget 100); carving 360 cells 0.54 ms (budget 10); a cold labelling of a merged floor 0.09 ms worst (budget 1, the four-dig number); 2,500 `GetApproachPoint` over merged floors 3.05 ms (budget 5); the 4,561-cell colony saves in 4.4 ms and loads in about 108 ms with its eight tunnels. The tunnel budgets are looser than "20 times a quiet run" asks (that would be about 30, 600 and 10 ms): the preflight was 33 ms before `GetTunnelUncarved` kept a count per floor version, and nothing here is on a per-step path with no tunnel.

**Playtest and checkpoints.** `RH_PLAYTEST_DISTRICTS=1 bash scripts/sim/rh_playtest.sh 110 d3_full guide full` is byte-identical to `Saved/RHPlaytest/c_full.txt` (1,468 lines, `cmp` clean, 0 sim errors). `c_full.txt` is the night-storage bot commit's final full run: written at 11:47, two minutes after the last edit of `RHPlaytest.cpp` (11:45, committed unchanged in `7f21c09`), by a sim that had not changed since D2, and its REPORT lines carry that commit's numbers (food 74.3 against 71.3 drawn, the bank empty 465 sol-hours, 18 banks). The bot fits no lift and orders no tunnel, so it never reaches the new code. `rh_checkpoints.sh all --force` re-made Midgame (sol 28.35) and Late (sol 62.35) at v45, and both load (`Colony loaded: slot 'Midgame'` and `'Late'`). The carving in the captures is `RH.TunnelNow` (the harness path); the Borer's own carving is the suite's.

**Known gaps.** No Borer anywhere means a stalled tunnel (the label says so); `RH.SinkNow` shafts have no Borer, so a tunnel to a dig served only by one is stalled until a Borer is sunk there. The TUNNELS card lists only the viewed floor's open tunnels; a cancelled one is a label on the floor, and a finished one is a corridor. Crew and robots still use the shaft head, as D2 left them. A tunnel cell can still be zoned by hand and so become a room (O1 stops only growth).

### D4, transit: lifts, capacity and robots below: what it settles, and where it deviated (2026-10-21, not yet committed)

Built from `docs/qa/2026-10-21/d4-transit-design.md`, in the order of authority the director set: the DIRECTOR ANSWER, then the REVISION (items 1-10 and the adopted optional items), then the PROFESSOR REVIEW, then the draft. **Q-A (a), Q-B (a), 2026-10-21:** the first lift car costs 400 kg of Struct (the config row `FirstLiftFitStructKg` stays at the price of every car, `LiftFitStructKg`); trams are deferred to D6 with people, so D4 builds no tram verb, record, edge, row, test or visual (the REVISION's "[Q-B D6]" branches throughout). Save **v46**, strict (D3 took v45); the default transit block is **12 bytes (0, -1, 0)**. With no lift car nothing moves: the battery is line-identical except the eleven lines that print the save version, and the new `transit.log`.

**What D4 builds.**
- **The gate is the colony's first fitted lift car** (`FitLift`, `Value` = the shaft). `HasTransit()` is "some shaft has a car"; with none, every line of D3 runs (the free, endless hoist, the instant head hand-off, every robot on the surface). The bill is `HasTransit() ? LiftFitStructKg : FirstLiftFitStructKg`, taken before the car is added, so the first fit's own take writes no ledger entry.
- **Capacity.** Every shaft has a places-a-sol capacity (an unfitted one is a 200-place hoist, a fitted one cars x 1,000). A place is one person or 100 kg of cargo; **a robot's body is 0 places** (REVISION item 4). Each shaft has an integer cargo ledger of three sol slots, written in any order, and its waits (`WaitSec`, `PrevWaitSec`) are frozen at a world-side sol threshold (`StepTransit`, straight after `StepUplink` in both bands). The roll reads the slot of sol s-2 and the people part from the commute flows, scaled by the ramp in permille (`2 x commuters x RampPermille / 1000`, floored once a shaft: 0 at 60 people or fewer, 1 place at 61, 320 at 160).
- **Every ledger event is stamped with its own step time** (item 2): a world-side bill (`TakeStructForBill`, growth bills, per source part to that source's serving shaft: O5) by `GetStepEndSeconds()`; a robot (boarding, the hand-off credit) by its own sub-step's end, `RH::RobotSubStepEnd` (one formula for both processors and the suite's driver). A robot reads a wait by that stamp with a 3.3 s guard after a boundary (`GetRobotWaitSec`), so robots-first and world-first batches of one frame read the same wait. `TransitUnstampedRobotWrites` counts the fallback and must stay 0.
- **The head hand-off pays its shaft** (item 6). A hauler that reaches a head with a cross-level drop holds there `10 x (wait + dwell + floors x seconds a floor)` sub-steps at idle draw and no wear (a runtime map in the sim, never saved, keyed to the task and the spot), then `HaulUnload(..., RobotLevel)` credits and charges the serving shaft the cargo's places. Both brains; both mid-delivery tests see the hold.
- **Robots below** (D3's design, carried): the deep planner (`PlanDeepRoute` over `FRHDeepNet`, wait-free; the robot's own waits are added at query time), rides (boarding ends a sub-step, the hold, `10 x (dwell + floors x 4)` ride sub-steps, Z flips at the end of the last), `RH::MoveAlong` routes through it with a target level, cross-level claims and the one ranking key (O9), the charge reserve below (`DeepReserveWh`: route-true, **every ascent hold priced at `MaxWaitSec`**, item 7), repair across floors for halted robots only, the load repair of robot levels (after floors, shafts, tunnels and cars are installed). **The first fit opens floor-level hauls to robots below** (no robot has ever claimed one; the fit alert says so, with the hoist's hold).
- **The commute.** With transit on, each underground end pays its shaft: `PerFloor(S)` (a hoist 6,000, a lift 2,000) plus its frozen wait at 100 cm a second. A trip between two floors is split over the shafts that reach the deeper one, **colonist k riding shaft R[k mod n]** (item 5, one pass); `PickCommuteShaft` adds the per-floor term (O4) and never a wait, so a crowd lengthens trips and never moves them (TR8b pins it). `FRHCommuteKey` gains `Lift` and `TransitWait`.
- **Save v46.** The transit block sits straight after the tunnel block: `LiftShaftCount` and (shaft, cars) each, `TransitCheckedSol` (-1 until the first fit), `LedgerCount` (0 with no car, else one a shaft) and per ledger the shaft, three (sol, kg, places) slots and both waits. A slot tagged `TransitCheckedSol + 1` is legal (a robots-first batch before the roll, saved between the two batches); a tag below -1, one whose `tag % 3` is not its slot, or one above +1 is refused. `SaveColony` now writes every robot's real `Level` (read from Z); a load keeps each robot's own level beside the spawn handles, never shifted by a row that is gone.
- **Presentation (placeholder, rows T1-T10 of `docs/qa/2026-10-15/gate-d-wording.md`).** A shaft card (cars, load against capacity, wait, "Fit a lift car (N kg Struct)" with the reason), a crowded-shaft line on the City panel, a fleet floor column and "N BELOW" badge, robots drawn only on their own floor, riders in a car drawn beside the crew's cab (inside its walls a rider could not be seen), a queue in front of the cab's door while holding. Console: `RH.FitLift [shaft] [now]`, `RH.Transit`, `RH.CamRobot ... [ride|below]`.

**Deviations, as the REVISION lists them.**
1. **Rovers never go below, and a rover gets no head hold** (O1). `IsFreightRoute`'s floor-drop refusal stays in every case; floor freight is hauler work (TR16b). No C6a or W1 line is touched (`RHSimRovers.cpp` gains only the mass balance's returned and lost counters).
2. **Trams move to D6 with people** (`going-deep-plan.md:38`).
3. **The waits (and `PrevWaitSec`) are saved.** The people part was read from the state at the threshold; re-deriving mid-sol would not reproduce it.
4. **Era teleports past lifts.** A robot hold or ride is not paid in era (robots park there); the world-side bills run in both bands.
5. **`CommuteShaftCmPerFloor` is re-described as the uncrowded hoist** (the value, 6,000, does not move).
6. **The gate is a player order** (the plan's "the ramp from 60 to 160 people stays" alone is not it). The ramp still scales every effect, now including the robot waits (item 8).
7. **The plan says lift load is a function of the commute flows only, never of robot positions** (`going-deep-plan.md:75`). D4 adds a cargo ledger, written by robot cargo moves and by bills. It stays integer, commutative and stamped by sim time, so it never depends on entity order. Without it the head hand-off would dominate for ever (D3 review item 8).

**Deviations from the REVISION's text (small).**
- **TR8b is new.** The REVISION says a wait-aware `PickCommuteShaft` fails TR8's monotonicity. TR8 as written runs floor-to-floor trips, which never call the pick, so running mutation 6 by hand showed the suite did not catch it. TR8b puts 700 floor-to-surface commuters on a crowded hoist beside a lift and checks four rolls: shaft 0 keeps 700 people and a 600 s wait every time (a wait-aware pick alternates 700, 0, 700).
- **TR18's `+1` slot** is made with `Debug_ChargeTransit` into the next sol's slot, saved, loaded and rolled against the never-saved twin, not by saving between the robot and world batches of the TR5 driver (the driver is local to TR5). The property pinned is the same: the file loads, the slot round-trips byte for byte, and the next roll equals the twin's.
- **The `lifts=1` bot orders an Infirmary module, not a Lab,** on a carved cell below (the Lab is locked behind research this early). The bot's own ledger cross-check is dropped (O7); it asserts mass conservation each sol (loaded = credited + returned + lost + in flight + returned by the load), `DeepOffLattice == 0`, no robot below at charge 0, every robot write stamped, one roll a sol, and the save/load keeping the (DefName, floor) multiset.
- **`FloorHeightMeters` at or under 1 m** logs an Error and uses 4 m (the REVISION says "asserts").
- **The riders' car is drawn beside the cab** (the first captures showed a rider inside the cab's closed walls, invisible). Presentation only.

**No new Mass fragment.** `RHAgentFragments.h` gains one field on the existing `FRHTaskFragment`: `int32 TargetLevel`, set beside every `TargetCm` write (the deep path's replan key is `(TargetCm, TargetLevel)`, O7). It is not saved (robot task state is never saved; in-flight cargo returns to its source at save), changes no archetype composition, and the explicit StateTree archetype is untouched (C4 item 4). The hold map, the reserve memo, `RobotPaths` entries and the deep net are sim-subsystem runtime state, rebuilt at load.

**Tests.** New fails-loudly `-transit` suite, **190 checks**, in `rh_battery.sh` (now 48 suites and 49 runs): TR0 the 16 rows read back from the live DT (drift fails loudly), the caps, the level from Z (Z 50 is 0, -350 is -1, rounded never truncated); TR1 two twins from one start, bit-identical up to the fit step (O6); TR2 the four `FitLift` refusals and the per-source bill charge (O5); TR3 the threshold in both bands, a three-sol jump, the slot tag, the era bill stamp, the robot wait guard; TR4 the wait by hand; **TR5 order independence: a batched-frame driver (K = 1, 7, 32 sub-steps x robots-first / world-first x entity order forward / reversed = 12 runs of 5 sols) with every slot, wait, delivery and position equal, plus the boundary case**; TR6 the people load (pick, ramp at 60 / 61 / 160, the k mod n split with each colonist's own distance, same floor, O4); TR7 the commute wait, the display mean, one rebuild per wait bump, 59 and 60 people; TR8 and TR8b a second car, a second shaft, a wait-free pick; TR9 the hand-off hold in a no-road and a road colony, idle draw, one credit, no hold with no transit, a same-level unload writing nothing, the seek rule; TR10-TR13 planner, ride, haul and build below, claims; TR14 and TR14c the charge reserve (the ascent hold at 600 s with wait 0; a threshold crossed on the walk); TR15 repair across floors; TR16b floor freight is hauler work; TR17 era parity with the people load at exactly capacity; TR18 the save (default block, a rider saved mid-ascent, the ring, the missing row, the ledger, the +1 slot, 19 refusals). **Mutation-checked, all ten of the REVISION's (item 2(e)):** (1) robot events stamped with the frame end: TR5's equality and its boundary case fail; (2) the roll reading slot s-1: TR5 equality and boundary (and TR3, TR4, TR7, TR10, TR11, TR14c, TR18 behind it); (3) a world-side bill stamped with the clock total: TR3's era case; (4) no lag guard on a robot's wait read: TR3's guard case and TR5's boundary case (not the five-sol equality); (5) a robot body charged one place: TR17 (and TR12a); (6) a wait-aware pick: TR8b (not TR8, see above); (7) a people load not scaled by the ramp: TR6's 61-person case; (8) an ascent hold at the frozen wait: TR14c (i) and (ii); (9) a hold with no `HasTransit()` test, and separately a same-level unload that writes the ledger: TR9; (10) a kept level appended for a record whose row is gone: TR18's missing-row case.

The new block (four cars on each of four shafts, 24 portals, 100 robots below, 5,417 colonists measured with transit on): one `StepTransit` roll 0.0003 ms (budget 1), a cold commute with transit on 5.5 ms (10), flows only 0.32 ms (1), a cold deep net 1.8 ms (3), 2,500 `PlanDeepRoute` calls 7.5 ms (10), 10,000 warm reserve reads 0.49 ms (1), 1,000 cold reserve reads 1.6 ms (10).

**Playtests.** `RH_PLAYTEST_DISTRICTS=1 bash scripts/sim/rh_playtest.sh 110 d4_full guide full` is byte-identical to `Saved/RHPlaytest/d3_full.txt` (1,468 lines, `cmp` clean, 0 sim errors), run again after the last source edit. `RH_PLAYTEST_DISTRICTS=1 RH_PLAYTEST_LIFTS=1 bash scripts/sim/rh_playtest.sh 40 d4_lifts guide full` and the same at 110 sols pass every `lifts:` check with 0 failures: the car goes onto shaft 0 at sol 11 (sol 11.72), 8 rides, 2 hand-off holds, 1 delivery below and 1 build completed below, 2 robots below at most, no robot ever below at charge 0, 0 off the lattice, 0 unstamped writes, one roll a sol (29 rolls in 29 sols at sol 40, 99 in 99 at sol 110), and the mass balance off by 0.0000 kg every sol (at sol 110: loaded 209,050.7 = credited 206,450.7 + lost 2,480 + returned by the save/load 120). The save and load at sol 11.77 (a Fabricator already below) kept every robot's floor. Captures, in `Saved/RHCapture/d4/keep` (all from `lifts_mid`, the bot's own save at sol 11.77, loaded in a real-renderer window): `d4_robot_riding_lift_car_floor-1.png` (a robot on the car's pale plate beside the crew's cab, in the second half of its ride, with the Fabricator working at the site above it), `d4_lift_car_plate_at_surface.png` (the first half of a ride: only the car's plate shows at the surface, the rider is below the ground; the weaker of the two), `d4_robot_working_floor-1_a.png` and `_b.png` (a Fabricator standing at an Air Filter site on floor -1).

**Known gaps.** The rider in a car and the queue are placeholder art (a pale plate and the robot, no car model). Holds and rides are not paid in era. A crowded shaft's wait reaches the commute and the robots a sol late (R3) and a robot sees a new sol's wait 3.3 s after the commute does (R14). A ride counted twice across a save is one place of over-count per robot in flight, not one in all: a load clears every robot's trip, so each robot in a hold or a ride at the save boards again and charges its cargo places a second time (a hauler's 200 kg is 2 places, a robot's own body 0), into the sol of the new boarding. Floors still have no cell for homes and seats (D6), so a tunnel or a second dig on one floor moves no commute number.

### Buttons fix and click test (2026-10-21, not yet committed)

Built from `docs/qa/2026-10-21/buttons-investigation.md`, which diagnosed why "none of the buttons worked" and designed the test. **Presentation only.** The sim, the save (v46), the data and every battery line are untouched.

**The bug.** `SRHMenus` is a full-viewport widget at Z 20, above the deck (Z 10), and its root took the Slate default `Visible`. Only its inner overlay collapses when no menu is open, so the root still swallowed every click, hover and wheel aimed at the deck, and each one fell through to `ARHPlayerController::OnClick` as a world click. Nobody had clicked the deck since the menus landed in `daaf86b` (10-15).

**Proved before it was fixed.** The click test was built first and run on the unfixed build: the title and pause menus passed (4/4 and 5/5 buttons reachable, NEW COLONY, CONTINUE and RESUME worked), and the deck failed with `REACH 0/65`, every miss `leaf=SRHMenus@RHPlayerController.cpp(56)`. 12 clicks reached `OnClick`, 2 of them on purpose. 16 of 26 steps failed. On the fixed build: `REACH 65/65`, 26 of 26 steps pass, 2 world clicks (both on purpose).

**The fix (4 changes).**
1. `SRHMenus` root `_Visibility = SelfHitTestInvisible`. The open screens keep their own `Visible` backdrops, so a title, pause or settings screen still swallows a click while it is up (the click test asserts all three).
2. `SRHCommandDeck` root the same, and `CloseMenus` restores `SelfHitTestInvisible`, not `Visible`. Only its panels and buttons take hits; the empty space between them reaches the world.
3. The deck now stops a press, release or wheel that lands on one of its panels and that no button took (`OnMouseButtonDown/Up/MouseWheel` return `Handled` on `SRHCommandDeck`; bubbling reaches a `SelfHitTestInvisible` root). Without this a panel click that no button takes bubbles up to the viewport and acts on the world: the click test with items 1, 2 and 4 but not this one fails `panel.blocks-world` and `leak.total` (a click on the OBJECTIVE title leaked). With it, clicking a panel no longer clears the selection or drops an armed build underneath.
4. `EndPlay` removes the Menus widget as well as the deck, so a level reload cannot stack a second one.

**The test (`Source/RedHope/Private/RHClickTest.cpp`, `scripts/unreal/rh_clicktest.sh`).**
- It walks the live Slate tree from the game window, arranging it top-down now (not reading each widget's last-painted geometry: a button scrolled out of its column keeps a stale position where another widget stands), and keeps only widgets inside every clip above them. It finds a button by its text, takes the centre, and asks `LocateWindowUnderMouse` what is there: `reached=YES|NO` and the leaf's type and creation site.
- It presses with real `FPointerEvent`s through `FSlateApplication` (move, down, up; a drag for the tunnel brush), so the press goes through hit-testing, capture and the viewport fallback like a hand. Two things were learnt the hard way: positions are desktop (tick) space, not paint space, and Slate re-sends a synthetic move to the OS cursor whenever the UI changes, which pulled the hover off the button before the release (a click needs `IsHovered()`). The test swaps the cursor user to Slate's faux cursor (`UsePlatformCursorForCursorUser(false)`) and places it at each press: the OS cursor never moves, and it swaps back at the end.
- `ARHPlayerController::WorldClickCount` counts `OnClick` calls. A button press that moves it leaked a click into the world and fails its step.
- Commands: `RH.Click <label> [nth]`, `RH.Hover <label>`, `RH.ClickSweep [why]` (`REACH n/m`, each miss with the widget in front of it), `RH.ClickText <label>`, `RH.ClickAt <fx> <fy>`, `RH.ClickWorld <Xm> <Ym>`, `RH.DragWorld <X0m> <Y0m> <X1m> <Y1m>`, `RH.ClickTest`. They queue and run a step at a time on the core ticker, so `-ExecCmds` can chain them.
- `RH.ClickTest` asserts: the title blocks a click; NEW COLONY; the deck sweep; an `OBJECTIVE` panel swallows a click; CONTINUE (`OnColonyReloaded` fires); the pause menu blocks, RESUME restores the speed it parked; the settings screen blocks; a BUILD tab opens the drawer, a card arms `SolarArray`, a ground click queues the Build; II, 3x, 8x, 60x, 1x set the clock; a manifest ADD (it meets Q1 first); Traffic on and off; the Tunnel button arms the brush and a drag queues a DigTunnel; the total of world clicks is exactly the two made on purpose.

**My own calls, for the director.**
- A dedicated script, not `rh_capture.sh`, because it needs `-RHTitle` and waits for a verdict line rather than screenshots. `rh_clicktest.sh` exits 0 only on `CLICKTEST RESULT PASS`. It parks the director's quicksave for the CONTINUE step and puts it back.
- The traffic legend is not asserted (the step checks the overlay flips). The first Power-tab and Solar Array card are pressed by their text, so a rename of either needs the script's label changed.
- A button that is greyed (disabled) is still counted by the sweep (geometry only) and skipped by `RH.Click` in favour of an enabled match.
- `RH.ClickText` and `RH.ClickAt` were added beyond the investigation's list, to assert that a panel and an open menu swallow a click.
- The test boots with `RH.Demo` and its sim state is throwaway; it does not touch `Saved/baselines`.


### D5 depth, depth has a price: what it settles, and where it deviated (2026-10-22, not yet committed)

Built from `docs/qa/2026-10-21/d5-depth-design.md`, in the order of authority the director set: the DIRECTOR ANSWERs, then the PROFESSOR RE-REVIEW conditions 1-5, then the REVISION (items 1-10 and the adopted O1-O8), then the PROFESSOR REVIEW, then the design. **Q-A (a), Q-B (a), Q-C (a):** shelter pays a Hope bonus capped at +3 for people homed below the cut-off, built like the window bonus; a cooling shortfall costs Hope only (up to -5) and is fully recoverable; the deep-home / surface-job net loss below -15 is accepted with no rule change (deep floors are for deep jobs). **The director's note, kept as asked:** Hope reaches arrivals and growth indirectly. Warmth costs Hope and nothing else directly, but like every Hope term it can slow settlers, births, the Big Arrival, disputes, discoveries, work tempo and city growth (new homes wait in the Failing band) while it lasts. No exemption is built: it would make warmth the only Hope term that immigration ignores.

**What D5 depth builds.**
- **The cut-off.** `DepthCostFromFloor` 5 (clamped 0..30). Floors -1 to -5 run today's code paths: every new branch tests `Level < -DepthCostFromFloor`, or a floor with cells below it, or a non-zero Warmth.
- **MaxDepth 20**, clamped to `RH::MaxDepthCap` 30 (the light key packs a bit a floor into a `uint32`).
- **Dig time.** An integer per-mille multiplier, `1000 + 150 x floors past -5`, fixed at batch start (bore, carve and tunnel cell by the floor the work is on; -6 is 13.8 h, -7 15.6 h, a carve on -6 3.45 h). Hydrogen and growth projects that carve scale with it. At or above the cut-off the batch hours and the fuel bill are the old expressions exactly (O1).
- **Cooling.** Every carved cell of a connected floor past the line (rated or not, tunnel cells included) asks `floors-past-the-line x 1 W` of the grid: one load entry a deep floor, `CoolingLoadPriority` 7, sorted after buildings and deepest first among equals. 100 cells on -6 are 100 W, on -10 500 W.
- **Warmth is driven by "the bank is scraping" (RE-REVIEW condition 1, the blocker's fix).** A step scrapes when (i) it starts with an empty bank and the full load is above generation (today's shed test) or (ii) the clamp would fire under the full load. The short floors are the cooling entries in the shed prefix of the priority walk over the full load, run whether or not the step actually sheds. A short floor warms at 1 / (`HeatRiseSols` x 1200) a second, every other floor cools at 1 / (`HeatFallSols` x 1200). Driven from the shed flag instead, Warmth never leaves its first step (about 8e-5 in the agent band): the -depth mutation shows it.
- **Hope.** `Shelter` = `HopeShelterMax` (3) x residents homed past the line / people, added straight after `Solidarity`; `WarmPenalty` = `HopeWarmMax` (5) x min(1, sum of residents x Warmth / people), subtracted straight after `CommutePenalty`. Both are +0.0 while nothing is deep or warm, so `Total` is bit-identical.
- **Save v47.** A warmth block straight after the transit block: a count, then (region, level, Warmth) per warm floor, shallowest first. The default colony writes 4 bytes. Structural refusals before any teardown; a level not past the cut-off (the row moved) is dropped with a Warning.
- **Presentation (placeholder, rows V1-V11 of `docs/qa/2026-10-15/gate-d-wording.md`).** The shaft strip's rows are in a scroll box capped at 11 rows (the SHAFT header and "Next shaft" above it, the lift card, the Tunnel brush and the tunnel list below it); choosing a floor scrolls its row into view; PageUp and PageDown step the view a floor (new Enhanced Input actions `IA_FloorUp` and `IA_FloorDown`). Floors past the line read their cooling (`-8 ▦30 ❄90 W`) and a warm one its share (` ≈14%`, amber text). The Bore button says its hours past the line. The folded POWER body reads "Cooling: N W for k deep floors", a circulator's card gains a DEPTH line, the CREW panel gains the Shelter and warm-floor lines. Floor tiles past the line darken a step per three floors and lerp toward a soft amber by Warmth, re-applied only when Warmth crosses a 10% step (the sim's `WarmthVersion`).

**Deviations.**
1. **Lift power is deferred to D6, with trams** (D4 R9, O2). A lift draw would move D4's -transit numbers and the `lifts=1` playtest on floors -1 to -5. *Owner settled (step-2 fixes, nit 15):* **D6e**, as the merged D6 design's section 5 says ("Lift power: built (D6e), with tram power"). This line and the D5 design said "D6" for the same family, and the commit said "D6e"; D6e is the gate, it is queued, and no gate has built it. No lift or tram line draws power today.
2. *Dropped* (the baseline radiation print is not bounded): the 15 added "radiation level -6..-20: 0.0000" lines are accepted (item 5).
3. **Cooling counts every carved deep cell, tunnels included** (O3): a 64-cell tunnel on -20 costs 960 W for ever.
4. **"Better shielded" is a Hope term, not a radiation number**: the index already reads 0.0000 from -4 down and flares spike only the surface.
5. **Warmth reaches settlers, births, the Big Arrival, disputes, discoveries, tempo and city growth through Hope** (item 2, R4, and the director's note above).
6. **Q-C (a): below -15 a deep home with a surface job is a net Hope loss.** With a lift car each floor adds 2,000 cm, so from -16 down a surface seat is long even on an empty lift (32,000 cm against the 30,000 line); Shelter (+3 by share) against the commute (-4, saturating at a long share of one half) loses for a colony of 160 or more. DP12 pins the numbers.
7. **A plateau, kept honest (RE-REVIEW item 1).** When the over-shed is large against the deficit (`r` between 1 and 2) a scraping cycle is three steps with two scraping, so Warmth holds; above 2 it falls. That follows the energy actually delivered, and the step pattern is the same in both bands while generation holds.
8. **Live-game agent band.** Robot charging takes from the bank outside the load list, and robots park in era, so a live agent band starts scraping slightly earlier than era does. The headless DP9 has no robots. Existing parity class; logged as a risk. *Its size (step-2 fixes, nit 9):* "slightly earlier" understated it. A 500 W charge pad drains about 10 Wh a sim-second, and at `HeatRiseSols` 1.0 every 120 s of earlier onset adds 0.1 Warmth, so one charging RC_E topping up 1,300 Wh at night can put the agent band about 0.3 Warmth above era, against DP9's 0.1 tolerance. The code is left as it is: draining the fleet's charge energy from the bank in `EraLogistics` would move era-band numbers, and wants its own gate with a DP9 variant that has a docking RC_E.
9. **The warm-alert latch is not saved** (step-2 fixes, nit 13). `InstallWarmth` re-arms it from the loaded Warmth, at 0.25 or more (`WarmAlertOn`), and nowhere else. A floor between 0 and 0.25 at the save is alerted again when it next crosses 0.25 after a load, and one at 0.25 or more is not alerted again. The alerts differ across a load; no sim state does, and the save format stays as it is.

**Deviations from the design's text (small).**
- **The shallow rows of the strip stay as they were.** SURF and -1..-5 are always visible (a dark row is solid rock); only rows past the cut-off follow "reached, next to bore, or viewed", so a fresh colony's strip does not change shape.
- **The strip's summary on the POWER tile is not extended** (it ran into the panel's title); the cooling readout is the body's own wrapped line, and the Hope card carries the Shelter and warm-floor lines.
- **`-depth` uses the real capacity rule.** A bed is a carved cell on a rated floor (x `ColonistsPerCell`), so DP7's twins run 130 residents on 130 cells with no free bed, not 20 on 130; DP9 runs 100 residents. The numbers in DP7 and DP9 are fixtures chosen so the scraping regime is the one the review analysed (DP9: six solar arrays, 500 W of cooling, the 2,000 Wh bank; the dusk onset falls 1,080 s and 1,093.8 s into the sol in era and agent).
- **`-districts` D4 asked for a floor that "does not exist" with `-6`**, which exists now: it asks for `-(MaxDepth + 1)`. The log line is unchanged. The REVISION's "no test bores past 5" missed it.
- **New test-only hooks:** `Debug_SetExtraGenW` (runtime, never saved, byte-equal saves asserted), `Debug_SetHopeWarmMax`, `Debug_SetFloorWarmth` (hand fixtures for the Hope read and hand-made saves only; the grid tests never use it), `Debug_ExtendShaftOf`, `Debug_StepPower`, `Debug_ApplyMaxDepthRow` and `Debug_ApplyDepthCostRow` (the load's own clamps).
- **`rh_clicktest.sh` gains `RH_CLICKTEST_PRE`** (console commands before `RH.ClickTest`).

**Tests.** New fails-loudly `-depth` suite, **109 checks**, in `rh_battery.sh` (now 49 suites and 50 runs): DP0 rows from the live DT, the caps, the line below `MaxDepth`, the radiation profile in `%.3e`; DP1 a twin with one carved -6 floor (every -1..-5 batch hour, floor air and rating, the literal `Cells x 1 W` check, the absolute 3.0 h pin for -5, B's `LoadW` equal to its building sum); DP2 bore -6/-7/-8 read from `BatchRemaining_h` (13.8, 15.6, 17.4 h; the hydrogen bill scales); DP3 a carve, a tunnel front and a growth floor-homes project (1.15 x `GrowthCellSols` on -6, unchanged on -5); DP4 the cooling load (100 cells on -6 add exactly 100 W, on -10 500 W, tunnel cells count); DP5 the shed prefix on a step that starts with an empty bank and on a rule-(ii) step; DP6 Warmth through the real grid (a Lander-only night, 1.0 after a sol and exactly 1.0 one step after it, recovery by `Debug_SetExtraGenW(250)`); DP7 no harm (twins: rating, air, beds, departure accumulators and the recall list equal, A's Hope lower, Oxygen deep, no Failing band, the alert copy passes the banned-word check); DP8 Shelter (10 of 40 on -6 is 3 x 0.25; on -5 it is +0.0); DP9 a paired 10-sol run (a daily reset to 0, a night peak of at least 0.25, dusk onsets within 120 s, Warmth at 0.95 within 0.1, Hope within 5%); DP10 the limits (-21 refused, -20 loads); DP11 the warmth block (byte layout, round trip, every structural refusal, the Warning repair, a v46 file refused); DP12 depth is far (120,000 cm with no car, 40,000 + wait with one, -15 at 30,000 not long and -16 at 32,000 long, a -20 seat 0). **Mutations, each run once by hand and restored by file hash:** DP1's off-by-one in the cut-off (`-L - DepthCostFromFloor + 1`) fails 17 checks including the absolute -5 pin and the literal `Cells x 1 W`; DP5's comparator without its tie rules fails the three shed-order checks; DP6's drive-Warmth-from-the-shed-flag fails nine checks: DP5's rule-(ii) check and the DP6, DP7 and DP9 checks (Warmth 0.000083 after a sol).

**Battery.** `bash scripts/sim/rh_battery.sh 10`: `BATTERY COMPLETE: 0 suite(s) not green`, 50 logs, all newer than both dylibs, nothing in Source or docs/data newer than `baseline.log`; both pins hold (`power: gen 50 W load 20 W`, `deposit Regolith_A: 40645 kg`). `python3 scripts/sim/diff_battery.py Saved/baselines/battery_post_btn Saved/RHBattery` shows exactly the REVISION's 45 lines: the 13 version lines (26 out and in: traffic 2, deep 3, framing 1, farice 2, shafts 1, tunnels 2, transit 2), the two range texts (`deep.log` K6 and `tunnels.log` TN8, "-1..-5" to "-1..-20"), the 15 added "radiation level -6..-20: 0.0000" lines, and the new `depth.log`.

**Playtests.** `RH_PLAYTEST_DISTRICTS=1 bash scripts/sim/rh_playtest.sh 110 d5_full guide full` is byte-identical to `Saved/RHPlaytest/bt_full.txt` (1,468 lines, `cmp` clean, 0 sim errors).

**-scale (outside the battery).** The five-floor numbers and digests do not move (digest `5f9d7352` at N=5000). The new 20-floor block (four shafts to -20, 12,600 cells below -5 rated, 15 cooling floors asking 100,800 W, 5,147 colonists of whom 2,000 live deep, the 100 robots re-spread over 20 floors): **three budgets fail, and they are findings for the director, not edits.** A cold deep net of 84 portals takes 5.6 ms (budget 3; the 24-portal net is 2.5); 2,500 `PlanDeepRoute` calls take 26 ms (budget 10; 8.2 at five floors); flows only with 2,000 deep residents take 1.03 ms (budget 1). Within budget: 1,000 cold reserve reads 8.2 ms (10), 10,000 warm 0.86 ms (1), a cold commute 6.5 ms (10), one threshold roll 0.0006 ms (1), `StepPower` with 15 cooling entries and a scraping bank 0.041 ms (0.05), `GetColonyHope` with Shelter and Warmth live 0.065 ms (0.5), save 5.1 ms and load 110 ms. One RC_H on -20 seeks the surface pad and docks after 1,100 sub-steps, never below 1,324 Wh, its reserve to the pad reading 336 of 1,500 Wh.

**Click test.** `rh_clicktest.sh` passes on the default colony (`REACH 65/65`, 26/26 steps, three runs; one early run failed "title.NEW COLONY: the title is still open", a timing flake on a loaded machine that passed on both reruns) and on a colony bored to -14 with `RH_CLICKTEST_PRE="RH.BoreNow 14"` (`REACH 70/70`, 26/26: Fit lift and Tunnel are reached with rows scrolled out).

**Checkpoints and captures.** `rh_checkpoints.sh all --force` re-made Midgame (sol 28.35) and Late (sol 62.35) at v47, and both load in a real-renderer window. Captures in `Saved/RHCapture/d5`: `d5_shaft_strip_20_floors_scrolled.png` (the scroll box at 20 floors, viewed floor -14, cooling on every row past -5), `d5_floor_cool_start.png` and `d5_floor_warm_amber_68pct.png` (a floor with the line moved to 0 by `-RHConfig=DepthCostFromFloor=0` so the lit demo floor is deep: before, and after its cooling went short, tiles amber and the row reading ` ≈68%`) and `d5_cooling_readout_power_panel.png` (the folded POWER body's cooling line).

**Known gaps.** The warm-floor tint is subtle at low Warmth and a deep floor is dark unless its circulator runs and lights are lit (a warm floor is in a brownout, so its lights are at 30%). The glyphs ❄ and ≈ are placeholders. A deep floor is not a place anyone is yet pulled to: Shelter is +3 at most and the commute can outweigh it (Q-C); D6's underground districts are the answer.

### D6a caverns: what it settles, and where it deviated (2026-10-22, not yet committed)

Built from `docs/qa/2026-10-22/d6-caverns-design.md`, section 1 of the MERGED DESIGN AND REVISION (every [RC n]), in the order of authority the director set: the DIRECTOR ANSWERs first. **Q-A (a):** reach, air, the Livable rating and the size minimum stay floor-wide through D8 (per-dig air and rating are deferred past step 2, not ruled out). **Q-B (a):** a cavern is up to 8 x 8 cells and 3 floors, carved with the ordinary 3 h `CarveCell` recipe; there is no new bill and no data row, so the limits are code constants (`RH::CavernMaxSide` 8, `RH::CavernMaxFloors` 3, `RH::MaxOpenCaverns` 2) and D6a touches no `docs/data` CSV or DataTable. **Q-C (a):** trams carry people only (D6d). This gate was started by one builder session and finished by a second; the second re-read every line against the spec (all items were present, one deviation fixed, see below).

**What D6a builds.**
- **`DigCavern` (Level = the top floor, Value = the number of floors, the two corners snapped by `CmToLattice`).** `CanDigCavern` is the preflight, run at queue time (the player controller's ghost uses the same function) and again at execute time, with all seven refusals each carrying a reason: (1) the deepest floor is reached by no shaft; (2) a side over 8, more than 3 floors, or below `-MaxDepth` (sides are checked in 64 bits, so an absurd point is a refusal, not an overflow); (3) a box point is a shaft column; (4) a box point is an uncarved point of an open tunnel or of another open cavern; (5) **one seed per floor**: on every floor of the box some uncarved point must be eligible; (6) more than 2 open; (7) every point already carved. `CanDigTunnel` refusal 6 and the sink-column check (`CheckSinkColumn`, SinkShaft refusal 8) gain "an uncarved point of an open cavern". `CancelCavern` (Value = Id) works like `CancelTunnel`: no new batch starts, carved cells stay, a batch in flight lands its cell. Ids are dense and never reused; `ShaftId` is fixed at execute time (the nearest shaft that reaches the bottom floor, ties to the lower Id, helper Borers count as shaft 0).
- **`FRHCavern`** (saved: Id, RegionId, ShaftId, Top, Bottom, Min, Max, bCancelled; derived and never saved: the uncarved count per floor keyed on `FloorCellsVersion`, and `bFinished`).
- **Eligibility is horizontal only (RC 2):** an uncarved, non-column point is eligible when it is 4-adjacent to a carved cell on its floor or to the column of a shaft that reaches that floor. Cells are never removed, so a point that is eligible once stays eligible.
- **One carve helper (RC 3), `CarveLatticePoint(Level, P)`:** refuses a column or a carved point before touching anything, removes the point from `Plan` (a carved point in the plan is a refused save), then appends through `AppendCarvedCell`. It is the only new path that appends a cell; ordinals stay add-only.
- **One predicate each (RC 1).** `RH::IsTunnelWork` (X is 2 or 3) and `RH::IsCavernWork` (X >= 100) replace every `X >= 2` reader: completion (cavern branch first, then tunnel), the batch-start log (caverns get their own line), `GetTunnelBatchesInFlight`, D5's batch-start dig pricing (a cavern batch reads its floor from `X = 100 + depth`) and its start-log branch. `grep -rn "\.X >= 2\|->X >= 2" Source/RedHopeSim` finds nothing (the five remaining hits for `X >= 20` are the suites' own lattice test).
- **`FindCavernWork`** runs after `FindTunnelWork` and only with a cavern on the list: open caverns by Id for this Borer's shaft, on the shallowest floor that has an eligible point and more uncarved points than batches in flight. **Completion** appends the eligible point lowest by (X, then Y) through `CarveLatticePoint`; if none is left it drops the spoil, adds no cell and prints a line.
- **Save v48.** A cavern block straight after D5's warmth block (NextCavernId, the count, then 40 bytes a record; the default colony writes 8 bytes), read into locals before teardown. Every structural refusal goes through `OutError` ("Save cavern list invalid: ..."), never an Error line: ids not ascending or not below the next Id, a region other than 0, a level outside `-MaxDepth..-1` or reached by no loaded shaft, a side or floor count over its limit, a box point on a column (a cancelled cavern is exempt: its rock may be sunk on later), two open caverns or an open cavern and an open tunnel sharing an uncarved point, a count over `MaxCavernRecords` or larger than the bytes left. The Warning repair: a work record with X >= 100 that names no cavern, or a floor outside its box, completes with spoil and no cell.
- **Presentation (placeholder; rows J1-J10 of `docs/qa/2026-10-15/gate-d-wording.md`, a fresh prefix).** `URHCavernVisualizerSubsystem` (a pure listener, `RedHopeSim` does not know it): the uncarved points of the box are a dashed teal ghost with a faint fill, the box a thin teal outline, a label over it; redrawn only when the cavern list, the floor table, the shaft list or the viewed floor changes. The Cavern brush (the shaft strip's Cavern button; a second press steps the height 1..3 floors, the press after the last disarms) is a box variant of the tunnel brush: the ghost runs the sim's own preflight, green accepts, red names the refusal, and the tip quotes the bill (cells, Borer-hours, spoil, O2 to fill). Under the button, the viewed floor's open caverns each with what is left and a Cancel. Console: `RH.DigCavern`, `RH.CancelCavern`, `RH.CavernNow`, `RH.CavernGhost`, and a line per cavern in `RH.Status`.

**Deviations.**
1. **The limits are code constants, not `RH_Config` rows** (Q-B (a), superseding the design's "Rows" line). No CSV, no DT sync.
2. **A work record of no known kind** (X from 4 to 99, or below 0; only a hand-made file can hold one) now completes with spoil and no cell and a Display line. No saved record can be of that kind, so every existing log is unchanged. *Corrected (step-2 fixes, nit 17):* this said "before D6a the final `else` carved a cell for it", which was true of only one of the two ranges. Before D6a (`9364a5d^`, `W:1542`) an X from 4 to 99 went to the **tunnel branch** (`Work->X >= 2`, a tunnel cell of the end `X == 3`, the Id in Y), not the carve branch; an X below 0 did reach the final `else` and carved a cell. That second change (a negative X now completes with spoil and no cell) was not logged until this note.
3. **The Warning repair fires when the batch lands**, not at load: the load keeps the work record as written and the completion branch finds no cavern (or a floor outside its box) and logs a Warning. The outcome the spec asks for (spoil, no cell) is the same; the colony is never torn down for it.
4. **Refusal 7 is tested before refusal 5**, so a box that is entirely carved says "already dug" rather than "touches no dig". All seven are still refusals with their own reasons.
5. **Test-only hooks:** `Debug_DigCavern` (the order without the uplink, same preflight and execution) and `Debug_CarveCavernCells` (the same completion a finished batch takes); both also back `RH.CavernNow` and the `-scale` block.
6. **No vertical travel inside a cavern until D7** (design deviation 2): people and robots change floors by shaft only.

**Tests.** New fails-loudly `-caverns` suite, **156 checks**, in `rh_battery.sh` (now 50 suites and 51 runs): CV0 the constants, the predicates and no data row; CV1 every refusal by its reason (refusal 5 included: a box over -3 and -4 beside a dig on -3 only is refused, the same box beside a reaching column is accepted, and a column that reaches only -3 seeds -3 but not -4), the two widened refusals, the uplink path, the execute-time race; CV2 the literal append order for a 2 x 2 x 2 box against an independent reference rule, 1,200 kg of spoil a cell against a twin, seven batches of 150 sim-s, no carved point left in `Plan` also through a save, and a full 8 x 8 x 3 box (192 cells); CV3 two Borers on two floors and an empty completion; CV4 a -5, -6 and -8 batch reads 3.0, 3.45 and 4.35 h; CV5 cancel with a batch in flight; CV6 tunnel 1 and cavern 1 at once; CV7 era against the agent band lay the same cells in the same order; CV8 the byte layout, the round trip, 22 hand-made files, a mid-batch save equal to a twin that never saved, both X >= 100 repairs, a v47 file refused; CV9 ordinal-to-point across a save and a load, and `FloorCellFallbacks == 0` over the whole suite. **Mutations (RC 1), each run by hand and restored by file hash, then rebuilt (the restored file's timestamp is old, so it must be touched before the rebuild; the first battery run after a restore without that ran the mutated binary and failed CV6, which is how it was found):** testing `X >= 2` before `X >= 100` at completion fails 20 checks (CV2, CV3, CV5, CV6, CV7, CV8, CV9: no cavern cell lands); keeping `X >= 2` in `GetTunnelBatchesInFlight` fails CV6 ("GetTunnelBatchesInFlight(1) = 3", the tunnel counts the cavern's batch).

**Battery.** `bash scripts/sim/rh_battery.sh 10`: `BATTERY COMPLETE: 0 suite(s) not green`, 51 logs, all newer than both dylibs' last change (RedHopeSim 14:37, RedHope 13:42, logs from 14:38), nothing in Source or docs/data newer than `baseline.log`; both pins hold (`power: gen 50 W load 20 W`, `deposit Regolith_A: 40645 kg`). `python3 scripts/sim/diff_battery.py Saved/baselines/battery_post_d5 Saved/RHBattery` shows 32 differing lines and the new `caverns.log` (863 sim lines): only version lines, 16 out and 16 in. deep 6 (K6 "file says version 47" to 48, K6 "hand-made v47" to "v48", K6 v46 refused /47 to /48), depth 4 (DP11 version 47 to 48 with the byte counts 2369 -> 2401 becoming 2377 -> 2409, the eight bytes of the empty cavern block; DP11 v46 refused), farice 4 (F16 two lines), framing 2 (e5), shafts 2 (SH12), traffic 6 (X15 two lines, plus the X16 line, whose only change is wall-clock "agent steps in 2.1 s" and "2.7 s" between the two runs), transit 4 (TR18), tunnels 4 (TN1, TN8).

**Playtests.** `RH_PLAYTEST_DISTRICTS=1 bash scripts/sim/rh_playtest.sh 110 d6a_full guide full` is byte-identical to `Saved/RHPlaytest/d5_full.txt` (1,468 lines, `cmp` clean, 0 sim errors).

**-scale (outside the battery).** The five-floor digests do not move (N=5000 `5f9d7352`, the same as D5). The new cavern block, run last on the 20-floor colony so no number above it moves: four 8 x 8 x 3 caverns, one beside each of the four shafts, carved to the end (768 of 768 cells, none open): carve 768 cells 1.95 ms (budget 20), 2,000 accepted full-box preflights 9.5 ms (budget 100), a cold dig labelling of a floor a cavern grew 0.108 ms worst of three (budget 1), save 4.8 ms, load 112 ms. **The three D4 20-floor budget failures are unchanged and are the next gate, not D6a:** a cold deep net 5.51 ms (budget 3), 2,500 `PlanDeepRoute` 25.5 ms (budget 10), flows only with 2,000 deep residents 1.023 ms (budget 1).

**Click test.** `rh_clicktest.sh` passes (29/29: `cavern.tool`, `cavern.drag` and `cavern.height` are new, and `leak.total` counts the cavern's drag as the third deliberate world click; `REACH 66/66` on the deck). Of three runs one failed `manifest.ADD` ("manifest has 0 line(s), expected 1"), a step that runs before any cavern step and that passed on both reruns: the known loaded-machine flake, not a D6a defect.

**Checkpoints and captures.** `rh_checkpoints.sh all --force` re-made Midgame (sol 28.35) and Late (sol 62.35) at v48, and both load in a real-renderer window (`RH.Load Midgame`: sol 28, 25 buildings; `RH.Load Late`: sol 62, 39 buildings; 0 sim errors). Captures in `Saved/RHCapture/d6a` (six frames from one run, `d6a_caverns_00..05.png`): the brush's drag ghost on a 6 x 6 x 3 box (green, the tip quoting "106 to carve, 318 Borer-hours, 127.2 t spoil, 10.6 t of O2 to fill"); the cavern ordered (a dashed teal ghost on every uncarved point, a teal box outline, the strip's CAVERNS card "#1 106 left, floors -1..-3, cancel"); floor -2 half carved (a dug hall beside the dashed cells still to carve, the card reading 56 left); the finished -1 hall and the finished -2 and -3 floors (the strip reading 42, 36 and 36 cells, the card gone).

**Known gaps.** The label over a box is a text component fixed to the default camera yaw (the tunnel label's convention): it reads edge-on from a rotated camera and can sit behind the right-hand panel. A finished cavern is an open hall with no vertical way through it (D7). The ghost's O2 figure is the whole box filled, not what the floor's filter can carry (R5): a weak filter's floor can flap while it is dug.

### Perf fix before trams: the three D4 budgets at 20 floors, a pure performance change (2026-10-22, not yet committed)

D6a's `-scale` run missed three D4 budgets at 20 floors (cold deep net 5.51 ms against 3, 2,500 `PlanDeepRoute` 25.5 ms against 10, flows only with 2,000 deep residents 1.023 ms against 1). This gate fixes all three with **no behaviour change**: no save bump, no data row, no budget number moved, nothing the suite measures changed, and none of the three forbidden shortcuts (a whole-query memo, all-pairs portal tables, A*) was used. Source touched: `RHSimTransit.cpp`, `RHSimCommute.cpp`, `RHSimWorldSubsystem.h`. Nothing in `Source/RedHope`, so no click test.

**What changed.**
