### E5 the Martian currency: what it settles, and where it deviated (2026-10-27, not yet committed)

Built from `docs/qa/2026-10-24/e5-martian-currency-design.md` on HEAD `99983b0` (save v55), in the order of authority the queue set: the DIRECTOR ANSWER (**Q-A (a):** issuing adds Earth tension +10 at once and lowers the requisition multiplier by 0.10 while the mark exists; **Q-B (a):** a Comply withdraws the mark and redeems every mark at the rate in force, with no spread and no cap - the loophole the re-review found (about +5,049 cr by a Comply against +3,409 cr through the desk) is accepted and no rule is added; **Q-C (a):** the bloc desk keeps 3% each way and takes at most 500 a sol each way) > `docs/qa/2026-10-26/preflight/ORCHESTRATOR-RULINGS.md` (rulings 1, 2, 6, 7, 8) > `CROSS-CHECK.md` section 6 (items 1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 10, 15, and section 5's per-pass order) > `E5-brief.md` > the design's REFRESH, re-review, REVISION and body. The brief's anchors were at `d842dd0`: every `Source/RedHopeSim` anchor still held at HEAD (the visual pass touched only `Source/RedHope`); `RHCheats.cpp` had moved by +12 lines and was re-anchored by symbol (`RH.Solidarity`, `RH.InjectRivals`), and `RHCommandDeckPanels.cpp` had not moved.

**Fix round (2026-10-27).** Read-only reviewers found five blocking problems and a list of minor ones in the first build; each is addressed (fixed in the code or the tests, or, for the findings about evidence and citations, corrected in this record), and the whole gate below was run again from scratch on the final build. Blocking: (1) CU8 checked R7's rule 2 for convoys only and the schedule broke it at the desk (the sol-28.5 exchange set aside 57.3% of the spendable marks after Zarya's sol-25.5 buy), and rule 3 was never asserted - Zarya's sol-25.5 order is now a net sale (the sol-28.5 exchange reads 504.682 marks held: 39.6%) and all three rules are asserted at the moment of each booking; (2) no fixture could tell the redemption's one floor from a floor per part - CU9's withdrawal now redeems a balance and an on-road owed amount whose remainders at the rate pass a whole milli-credit together; (3) the Comply's and the Pacify's journal effects were pinned nowhere and CU8's table expectations were unasserted - each act's entry is now checked against hand-written values and CU8 asserts every row of its table; (4) the TRADE tooltip still said the standing trade settles in credits - it now names the currency (W2 and W4 changed: gate-d-wording Y16); (5) DefyIssue, and a Comply while the mark exists, raised two alerts in one tick and the banner (it keeps only the last) hid Gate D's D44 and D43 - each act now raises one alert. The minor findings are listed where they land below.

**Fix round 2 (2026-10-27).** The re-review found three more blocking problems, each fixed, and the whole gate was run again from scratch on the final build (the pre-change `-scale` figure reused as recorded; no old binary reinstalled). (1) CU10's fresh-load `NextCommandId` check could not fail without the desk orders' raise (the journal's acts were the newest commands in its fixture): CU10 now also loads `cu10cut`, whose newest command is a pending desk order (#66 past the journal's #65), and asserts the counter is exactly one past it - mutation 16 deletes the desk raise and fails it. (2) R7 O8's exception (`!bIssueEntry`), which keeps every save made between an issue act and its issue roll quiet, was never loaded: CU10 now saves and loads both consistent pre-roll states - a stand-alone Issue (it loads with no Warning, still issued, and the next roll issues the market at 1000) and DefyIssue with a Comply in one sol (it loads with no Warning, the withdrawal still pending, and the roll issues and withdraws at 1000) - and mutations 17 and 18 delete the exception from each rule and fail them. In play this window is every autosave in the sol after an issue (the capture's own log shows one). (3) The DefyIssue alert (D44 + Y10's clause, about 270 characters) ran under the right column and off both window edges: the deck's alert banner now wraps at the gap left of the right column (`SRHCommandDeck::GetAlertWrapAt`, in local units from the deck's width: 1,026 units at 1440x860, so the pill ends about 27 px before the column), centred, and the DefyIssue banner, the stand-alone issue's and `THE MARK IS RETIRED` are captured within their hold. Minor findings fixed in the same round: the ToCredits desk payout is computed as `q x Keep + floor(m x Keep / 10^7)` with `Paid x r = q x 10^7 + m` (the same single floor, no int64 overflow inside the config clamps) and a loaded desk order above `DeskCapPerSol` is dropped with a Warning; CU10 pins the exact Warning set of the dropped-convoy fixture (E4's drop and the reserved-marks counter, two in all), reads both lists back ascending with two entries each, doctors each repair's other direction (a rate below the band, the tension record below its range, a repeated journal key, a repeated member) and the over-cap desk order; CU4's Greed check says what it tests; the Dilemma card says a Comply withdraws the mark while it exists (Y17); the TRADE tooltip names a currency only while an order could be booked, and gives the refusal while a withdrawal is pending (Y16); the issue tooltip names the threshold only when the reason is not the identity refusal, in one spelling (Y2); the desk line names both units and SEND comes before CLEAR (Y4); `RH.Currency status` says `the market has not issued the mark` instead of `no close since the load` when there is no market issue (Y13); Zarya's Notes name the converted settlement too (`DT_Rivals` re-synced, rows 2 -> 2).

**What E5 builds.** (Placeholder name: the mark. Every number below is a placeholder awaiting the director.)
- **Two issue acts, both uplink orders.** `Currency Issue` (the live identity at least `CurrencyIssueIdentity`, 20) and `Solidarity DefyIssue` (the whole Defy, then the issue, at any identity; checked whole before the Defy runs, so it is never half done). One rule, `CanIssueCurrency(bDefyIssue, Why)`, for the verb, the deck and the cheat, in the brief's order: already issued; being withdrawn; no neighbor with an economy and an open route whose `BlocStance` the identity after the act meets (R7 O1); and, for the stand-alone issue only, the identity. At the act: tension +10 (clamped), `bCurrencyIssued`, `=== CURRENCY ISSUED: ... ===` and **one alert for the act**: the stand-alone issue raises its own (`A MARTIAN CURRENCY — ...`); `DefyIssue` raises the Defy's, Gate D's D44 kept whole with the issue's clause after it in the same alert (`ResolveDilemma(Cmd, false, Why, bIssuing)`; `IssueCurrency(Cmd, Sub, bAlert)`). Nothing else. `GetRequisitionMultiplier` keeps HEAD's statements and subtracts `CurrencyRequisitionPenalty` only inside `if (bCurrencyIssued)` (CU1b pins the unissued value bit for bit).
- **The political record** (`IdentityViewMilli`, `TensionViewMilli` and a journal) runs from sol 0 in every colony, issued or not, outside `RollMarket`'s live gate, and logs nothing. Each act's worker appends its entry keyed by its own `ExecuteAt` (`ClearSol = floor(ExecuteAt / 1200)`, then `CommandId`, then sub): a Defy or a Comply sets the identity record to the live value it left and adds its nominal tension delta (-12000 / -45000), a Pacify -20000 and no identity, the issue +10000, a withdrawal nothing. `ResolveDilemma` and `ResolvePacify` now take the executing `const FRHCommand&` (cross-check item 8). At each roll the record walks the sols the pass closes one at a time: that sol's drift (600, only while a row is `SliceActive`), then every entry due by it, clamping the tension to [0, 100000] after each. A pass closes one sol except a long jump's last pass, which walks every sol it skips the same way, so a harness jump leaves the record that closing those sols one by one would leave, clamps included (a 12-sol jump adds 12 drifts; the rate still steps at most once a pass). In play these are the only writers of the live identity and tension (the start, the drift, a Defy, a Comply, a Pacify and the issue), so the record follows the live values; a harness write (`RH.Earth`, `Debug_AddTension`) is not seen, by design.
- **The market's issue** happens at the roll closing the act's sol s: `CurrencyIssueSol = s`, `CurrencyOpenSol = s + 2`, the rate at `CurrencyRateStartMcr` (1000), the first step at the roll closing s + 1. Admissions run from the issue roll on: `RivalEconomies` walked by name string, a rival with an economy joining when `0 <= BlocStance <= record / 1000`, from the sol closed + 2 (`Bloc: %s joins the mark ...`). Zarya stands at 20 and Meridian at 40 (`RH_Rivals.BlocStance`, after `StoreCap`); a row built without the column is -1 and never joins; a Greed-shaped rival (Zarya's row copied, as the commandlet builds its Greed) keeps the 20 it was given but has no economy and never joins.
- **The rate** (milli-credits per mark, `RateMcr`) is 3.3's integer update, every signed division through `MarketDivTrunc`, read from the record and the bloc tally (only `bMarks` orders enter the tally; desk flows never do), never the live axes. It is clamped to [250, 4000] and does not step at the withdrawal roll.
- **Settlement.** An order's currency is fixed at booking from its clearing sol `EffSol = max(floor(ExecuteAt / 1200), MarketCheckedSol)`, computed after E4's catch-up: a member from that sol settles in marks (E4's reservation read in milli-marks against the spendable marks, `MarkSpend` at the clearing, `OwedMmk` paid through `MarkPay` at the return); any other priced order after the market's issue is a credit order whose net, in the market's unit, converts at the rate in force during the clearing sol (the charge rounded up, the owed rounded down; its reservation `ceil(sum buy x Hi(q) x HiR(r) / 1000) - floor(sum sell x Lo(q) x LoR(r) / 1000)`); before the open sol every order is E4's, exactly. The standing trade is booked like any priced order, so it settles the same way. `ValueMcr` and the goodwill floor stay in the market's unit for every order. Purses: `MarkBalanceMmk == MarkEarnedMmk - MarkSpentMmk`, `MarkPay` / `MarkSpend` the only writers.
- **The desk** (`Exchange`, Target `ToMarks` / `ToCredits`, Value whole units): `CanExchange(Dir, Amount, EffSol, Why)` in the brief's order; the amount reserved in its source purse; cleared in `RollMarket` after the convoys, by Id, at the rate in force, each payout one floor of the exact rational with the 3% spread (the marks-to-credits payout split as `q x Keep + floor(m x Keep / 10^7)` with `Paid x r = q x 10^7 + m`, so no product leaves int64 for any config inside the clamps: a desk order is at most 1e9 mmk and the rate at most 1e8 mcr); `DeskCreditMcr` counts the credit it pays in.
- **The withdrawal** (Q-B a). A Comply while the mark exists ends the penalty at once (`bCurrencyIssued` false), sets `bWithdrawPending` and journals a Withdraw entry; it raises one alert, Gate D's D43 unchanged (the withdrawal's start is a log line, and the deck's status line says the market is closed). From then booking refuses every priced order and every desk order with `The market is closed while the mark is withdrawn` (a barter convoy is still allowed). The first roll at or after the Comply's sol with no uncleared priced convoy and no pending desk order, after the clearing and the reprice: one floor over the balance plus every on-road owed mark at the rate in force (1000 when the issue and the withdrawal share the roll), paid through `CreditPay` into `CurrencyRedeemedMcr`; the purse emptied, the on-road convoys owing nothing more; every slot of every region re-expressed and held in E4's band; the bloc and the tally reset, the rate and the sols reset; no admission and no rate step at that roll; the alert `THE MARK IS RETIRED — ...`.
- **Save v56**, the `'RHE5'` frame written straight after `'RHE4'`, laid out as brief section 5 (238 payload bytes for a colony with an empty journal: a 258-byte frame). `ReadCurrencyBlock` never refuses; it repairs with Warnings (a rate or record out of range clamped; an unusable journal emptied and the record read from the live values; unsorted members reset; a mark order whose convoy E4's reader dropped dropped silently, one naming a convoy E4 never held or disagreeing with it dropped with a Warning; a negative purse 0 with spent following; desk orders that cannot stand dropped, among them one above `DeskCapPerSol`, which booking never sets aside) and raises `NextCommandId` past every pending desk order and journal entry (trap 7; CU10 shows each raise on a fixture where it is the newest command). `ReconcileReservations()` runs after it: E4's lifetime-sales clamp and reservation recompute moved there from `ReadMarketBlock` with E4's texts, conditions and order (`market.log` keeps its 47 `LoadColony v55:` Warnings, line for line), the credit sum now including the pending ToMarks amounts and the cut taking desk orders by Id descending before convoys; the same for marks; E5's two lifetime totals clamped first (the redeemed before the desk's); then the flags: R7 O8's three reconciliations and a fourth (both flags up: the withdrawal stands and the mark is not issued), silent on the consistent files saved between an issue act and its roll (Earth's flag up, or a withdrawal pending, with the Issue entry still in the journal). A lost or malformed `'RHE5'` starts the mark unissued with a Warning; a market block started at its start values (`ERHMarketBlockLoss` Lost / Malformed / SlotsReset / RegionsRepeated, cross-check item 3) drops the frame too, with a Warning only when the frame held something to lose (ruling 2). `bMarketBlockLost` and `bCurrencyBlockLost` are transient.
- **E8's reads** are declared under E8's names (cross-check item 10): `GetCurrencyIssueSol`, `GetCurrencyOpenSol`, `IsMarketInMarks`, `GetRateInputsBp(int32&, int32&, int32&)` (the last step's inputs, from the record and the tally), `GetDeskBooked(Sol, ToMarks, ToCredits)`, `GetDeskOrders(TArray<FRHDeskOrder>&)`, `GetMarkOrder(ConvoyId, Reserved, Owed)`, with `IsCurrencyIssued`, `GetRate`, `GetBlocMembers`, `GetMarketBlockLoss` and `GetCurrencyBlockBytes`.
- **Presentation (minimal; E8 is the full panel).** The Dilemma card's third button `DEFY, AND ISSUE A MARTIAN CURRENCY` (greyed by the rule, the reason in its tooltip and on a dim line under it; its tooltip says the tension ends 10 higher than a plain Defy leaves it, the number read from the config); EARTH & NEIGHBORS gains `ISSUE A MARTIAN CURRENCY` before the issue (its tooltip names the +20 threshold and the +10 tension from the config), and after it a MARTIAN CURRENCY block (status, `Credits / Marks` with spendable and set aside in the tooltip, `1 mark = %s cr` with the last close's step, or "set at the issue", or "no close since the load", the desk with its spread and cap, `To marks +100`, `To credits +100` netting against a draft the other way, `CLEAR`, and SEND read by `CanExchange`); each neighbor's market card gains the bloc line under its MARKET heading (`Bloc member` / `Joins the bloc at identity %+d`, and the currency an order sent now settles in, shown only while an order to it could be booked); a market card counts in marks once the market does; the TRADE tooltip names the currency the standing trade settles in under the bloc line's guard, and the Credits bar's tooltip counts exchanges once one is pending (W2, W4: Y16); the Dilemma card's prose says a Comply withdraws the mark while it exists (Y17); the alert banner wraps at the gap left of the right column. `RHOrder` words: `Issue` + `a Martian currency (the mark)`, `Bloc exchange` + `credits to marks` / `marks to credits`, and `Defy, and issue a Martian currency` after HEAD's `Answer Earth`. Console `RH.Currency [status] | issue | desk ... | grant ... | deskdraft ...` and `RH.Solidarity defyissue`.

**UNCERTAIN items of the brief, each with its stated default taken.**
- **M8, the DefyIssue order:** the demand is checked first, with HEAD's text (`There is no demand to answer right now.`), then `CanIssueCurrency(true)`; a failure leaves nothing done.
- **M6, a long jump's last pass:** the sol being closed for the transitions and the admissions is `SolNow - 1` (the pass's `DueUpTo`); the record walks every skipped sol there, one at a time (the fix round interleaved each sol's drift with its entries, as closing the sols one by one would).
- **The deck's and the cheats' preview sol:** `CanPlaceOrder`, `CanPlaceStandingOrder`, the desk preview, the bloc line and the TRADE tooltip read `GetBookingSolNow() = max(floor((now + the order lag) / 1200), MarketCheckedSol)`; at the margin the preview may differ from the booking, which is colony-side.
- **CU8's sol-29.5 Kestrel order** is a net sale (its mark reservation 0).
- **Trap 11, the desk payout:** one floor of the exact rational each way (`floor(A x 1000 x (10000 - S) / (r x 10000))`, `floor(A x r x (10000 - S) / (1000 x 10000))`), in the code and in CU6's reference. Only the credits-to-marks payout can tell one floor from two (whole marks times the rate are whole milli-credits before the spread); CU6's rate, 1.036, is one where it does: 280.888 marks, where flooring twice gives 280.887.
- **M9, a barter convoy during a pending withdrawal:** allowed; the withdrawal waits only for priced convoys and desk orders.
- **B2 / ruling 2, the lost-block Warning:** printed only when the `'RHE5'` frame held something to lose; otherwise the currency starts unissued silently (MX9's lost, malformed and slot-table fixtures add no line to `market.log`).
- **Trap 8, the lifetime clamp's precedence:** each of the desk and redeemed totals in [0, Awarded - SalesPaid] first, the redeemed cut before the desk's if together they pass it, then E4's own clamp of the market sales in what is left (byte for byte E4's with E5's totals at 0). The brief's "then the desk credit" cut is kept but cannot fire: after each total is clamped into the room, their overrun is never more than the redeemed total.

**Deviations** (the spec is silent or contradicts itself, or the build found a better shape):
- **The desk's text while a withdrawal is pending.** The brief contradicts itself: its binding 10 (from REVISION R5, which outranks the design's 2.5) says booking refuses every priced order and every desk order with `The market is closed while the mark is withdrawn`; its 3.3 `CanExchange` list, copied from 2.5, has check 2 `The mark is being withdrawn` (`!bCurrencyIssued`). The first build took 3.3's text; the fix round follows binding 10: check 2 is `bWithdrawPending || !bCurrencyIssued` with R5's text, so a convoy order and a desk order get one text, and `The mark is being withdrawn` is now only the issue's refusal (CU9 pins both).
- **One alert per act** (the deck's banner keeps only the last alert, `RHColonyVisualizerSubsystem::HandleAlert`). `DefyIssue` raises D44's text whole followed by Y10's issue clause; the Comply that withdraws the mark raises D43 alone (the first build's `THE MARK IS WITHDRAWN` alert is gone); the issue's own alert only for the stand-alone act. D44's clause is flagged for the Gate-D review. A residual, as with any two alerts: in the era band the withdrawal roll can share a step with a Comply that runs in the last minute of its sol, and `THE MARK IS RETIRED` then replaces D43 on the banner.
- **A late Issue entry.** The market's issue sol is the sol the roll closes when it applies the Issue entry (equal to the act's sol in play; later only when E4's catch-up closed that sol before the act ran, R7 O18), so the two-sol lead always holds.
- **The desk's first refusals.** `CanExchange` first refuses an amount that is not 1 to 1,000,000,000 whole units (`An exchange is a whole number of %s, at least 1`); with no market issue it says `The desk opens at the end of sol %d` only when an Issue entry is pending (the sol it will open, `max(the entry's sol, the open sol) + 2`) and `No Martian currency is issued` otherwise. Unknown targets: `No such currency order '%s'`, `No such exchange '%s' (ToMarks or ToCredits)`.
- **Every config row is clamped** through one function, `CurrencyConfigClamp`, which CU0 drives with out-of-range values: beyond the brief's five ranges, identity -100..100, tension 0..100, the penalty 0..1, the start rate 1..1,000,000, the weights and the gain 0..1000, the floor 1..9999 and the ceiling 10001..1,000,000 bp, the spread 0..9999, the cap 0..1,000,000. The rate's floor is at least 1 mcr, so no conversion divides by 0.
- **The bloc tally** advances every roll in every colony and is reset at the withdrawal; with the harness switch off it neither advances nor resets.
- **A withdrawal with no market issue** (only a doctored load can reach it) redeems at the start rate and re-expresses no price. The withdrawal also holds `bCurrencyIssued` false itself, whatever a file said.
- **Load repairs beyond section 5's list**, each with a Warning: the issue and open sols disagreeing; the withdrawal's sol not fitting its flag; the bloc tally out of range; members, mark orders or desk orders held with no issued market; a desk order malformed; a cleared convoy still holding marks (released); both flags up (the withdrawal stands; play never writes it). A mark order naming an Id that E4's block never held warns; one whose convoy E4's reader dropped is silent (cross-check item 2).
- **`bCurrencyBlockLost` means "the frame could not be used"**: lost, malformed, or dropped because the market block was lost.
- **`GetRateInputsBp`** returns the inputs as they stand after the last roll, which are the last step's (a step is a roll's last write). The last step itself is transient (`GetLastRateStepMcr`), and `GetRateStepNote` says what it means - 0 no close since the world started or the colony loaded, 1 the issue set the rate, 2 the last close stepped it - so the deck never calls an unknown step "unchanged" after a load.
- **Transient records for tests and the deck:** `FRHMarketClearing` gains `Currency`, `RateMcr` and `SettledMcr`; a new `FRHDeskClearing` (64 kept). Never saved.
- **New log texts in marks** for a mark order's and a converted order's clearing, and the withdrawal's alert at its roll; E4's texts are untouched for E4's orders.
- **The deck** shows the bloc line only from the market's issue, on the market card the brief names (`BuildMarketCard`, so only for a neighbor with an economy while the market is live, and only once it is available), and its "settles" clause only while an order to that neighbor could be booked; the issue button only while a neighbor trades in a market; the currency block only once the mark exists. The desk's draft nets like the market card's (a press against its direction takes 100 off it) and has a CLEAR after SEND, as the market card orders them - neither is in the brief.
- **The bloc line's wording.** The spec's `Joins at identity +40` reads `Joins the bloc at identity %+d`, and the line has two states the spec does not name: `Bloc member from sol %d` (admitted, trading in marks from a later sol) and `Stays out of the bloc` (a stance of -1). Y5 lists them. The identity is not shown as a number anywhere on the deck (only `The colony leans ...`), so this number and the issue tooltip's are flagged in Y2 and Y5 for the director or E8.
- **The alert banner wraps** (fix round 2). The banner is one `STextBlock` centred in the deck's overlay; the DefyIssue alert (D44 whole + Y10's clause) is about 270 characters, so it now wraps at the gap left of the right column, in local units from the deck's own width, centred; every shorter alert is one line as before. One more consequence of one banner, recorded for the director: a DefyIssue nets -2 tension (placeholder numbers), so answering at 62 or more raises the next demand in the same tick and its `EARTH TRANSMISSION` alert replaces D44 + Y10 (a plain Defy does so from 72); the capture answers at 61.
- **E4's W2 and W4 change with E5** (gate-d-wording Y16): the TRADE tooltip names the currency the standing trade would settle in (W2's text word for word when it is credits as at E4), and the Credits bar's tooltip says "market orders and exchanges" while an exchange to marks is pending. Zarya's Notes in `RH_Rivals.csv` say so too (synced to `DT_Rivals`).
- **`CanIssueCurrency`'s partner test** reads the identity after the act as the record will (whole points, truncated), so at least one rival joins at the issue roll whenever the act is allowed (in play; a harness write to the live identity can make the two differ).
- **Spelling.** `No neighbour would join the mark` keeps the design's British spelling; the rest of the new copy follows the game's "neighbor".
- **MX1 and XE10.** MX1's compare is bounded to the market frame's own bytes (the printed text unchanged); XE10 requires the market frame's footer to meet `'RHE5'`'s header (found by tag), never "the last frame".
- **QA hooks** (runtime only, never saved): `Debug_DiscoverRival`, `Debug_SetNextCommandId` / `GetNextCommandId`, `Debug_StepRate`, `GetCurrencyConfig`, `GetDeskClearings`, `GetRateStepNote`, `GetCurrencyIssueIdentity` / `GetCurrencyIssueTension` (the deck's tooltips); the deck's `Debug_SetDeskDraft` and `ARHPlayerController::Debug_DeskDraft`; `RH.Currency grant` and `deskdraft` (DEBUG/QA, for captures).
- **One click step the brief does not name.** The brief (its section 7) says: "The spec adds no click steps. Optional: one step that sees the Dilemma card's third button (greyed, with a reason) and one that sees the desk's SEND." The build added neither of those two but `currency.ISSUE`, after `market.SEND`: the harness moves the identity to +20 and a real press on ISSUE A MARTIAN CURRENCY must leave a `Currency Issue` order in the queue (the first record said the brief "allows one"; it names two, and this is a third shape). The click test is 93 steps. The greyed third button with its reason and the desk's SEND (greyed with its reason, and enabled) are covered by the captures instead.

**Test-side choices.**
- **CU8's grid.** Every order and act is enqueued on the era twin's grid (30 s past each minute), so its `ExecuteAt` (enqueue + 45 s) is the same in both twins: the "boundary + 10 s" items execute at boundary + 15 s and the ".5" items at x.5 sol + 15 s, with the brief's semantics (on the era step that crosses the boundary, before its roll; after the agent band's roll). The bar holds every home sol equal except the one the re-review designed to split: Zarya's sol-31 convoy is home in sol 34 in the era twin and in sol 35 in the agent twin, and the redeemed credit is equal.
- **CU8's schedule and R7's rules.** Zarya's sol-25.5 order is a net sale (`Struct:-60`, the reviewers' fix), so every mark reservation in the run is 0 but the sol-28.5 exchange's, which reads 504.682 marks held (200 = 39.6%). The three rules are asserted at the moment of each booking, from `OnCommandExecuted` (the amount is already set aside, so "the spendable before it" is the spendable plus the amount): rule 2 for every convoy and every desk order in its own purse; rule 3 for every mark booking, against the marks held without the owed marks of any convoy home within the last sol (a convoy due home within the next sol has not paid them in yet). What rule 3 reads is band-exact, so it is compared across the twins too.
- **CU8's table.** Each order's currency is read from its clearing (Kestrel's sol-23 and sol-27 orders converted, Zarya's sol-25 and sol-31 orders and Kestrel's sol-29 order in marks); a quarter sol after the `+25` write Earth embargoes Zarya and the third demand is pending; the record after the write equals the record after the roll closing 29, and the roll closing 30 moves it by exactly 600 - 20000; the Pacify leaves the demand pending; every slot's price and every economy rival's store of every slot are traced at every threshold.
- **CU7's sync case runs in two orders.** The brief's (a +10 shift, a sync, a Defy) cannot fail mutation 13; the second (a sync, then the +10 shift, then the Defy: a harness write between the sync and the act) carries it. Its jump fixture (an act due inside a jump's last pass) is a doctored file: no step can journal an act whose sol a later roll will skip over.
- **CU9's one-floor fixture.** The purse is topped up by the fewest milli-marks (1 with today's prices) that make the balance's and the on-road owed amount's remainders at 3.499 pass a whole milli-credit together; the check asserts that the fixture bites before it asserts the one-floor value. CU9's live-market twins (a roll without a Comply, and the same roll with one) read the reprice the withdrawal re-expresses.
- **CU10's doctored lengths** (a truncated `'RHE5'`, a region written twice in `'RHE4'`) rebuild the frame around the new payload (`CurSuiteReframe`: header, CRC and footer rewritten), since `RHSaveFrame::Reseal` rewrites only the CRC.
- **CU1's logs** are compared with every `(N bytes` masked, as `diff_battery.py` masks them: an autosave mid-run measures the `'RHE5'` frame, whose journal (sized by history) only the on twin keeps between an act and its roll.
- **CU2's ceiling.** With the shipped weights no step rises past a rate of 2.4 (the anchor outweighs the best trade and identity), so the 4000 ceiling is pinned by the band's clamp (`Debug_SetRate(5000)` reads 4000) and a step down from it; the 250 floor is reached by a step. `StepRate`'s own clamp to the ceiling is therefore not driven by a step (the re-review accepted this as recorded; there is no hook to raise a weight).
- **CU9's wait** is built on a doctored, resealed file (the convoy's clearing sol one past the roll, the withdrawal pending): in play every order booked before the Comply clears by the Comply's own roll and none can be booked after it, so no unedited fixture reaches the wait.
- **Fixtures that roll the market ahead of the clock** (`Debug_RollMarket`) bring the clock up to the market's open sol before a save, or E4's own repair resets an open sol ahead of C5d's on load.

**Findings.**
- **A restored mutation can leave its object behind.** The CU13 driver restores a file with a copy that keeps the original's modification time, which is older than the object compiled from the mutation, and UBT decides by time: the next incremental build would keep the mutated object. Found in the first round after mutation 1; the driver touches every restored file, and the final binary was built twice from the restored, touched sources (`-DisableAdaptiveUnity`, then the normal build).
- **The machine was loaded by other work throughout** (other jobs, the data volume at 97-98% full, and in the first round a job that deleted files under `/private/tmp`, that round's scratch logs among them: the pre-change `-scale` raw logs, the per-mutation build and run logs and the build logs; its first gate battery was also aborted by a DerivedDataCache write failure at that time). Not this gate's processes; none was touched. The pre-change `-scale` figures survive only in the builder's notes, written before the first E5 source edit and kept at `Saved/RHCapture/e5/gate/NOTES.md`. This round keeps every log on the project volume (`Saved/RHCapture/e5/`).

**Tests.** New `-currency` suite (`RHSimCurrencySuite.cpp`, in `rh_battery.sh` after `-market`: 58 suites, 59 runs), **63 checks** (51 in the first build, 59 after the first fix round), each with its conditions tried in order so a failure names the first that failed (`[first failing condition: #n]`); no path logs an Error on purpose:
- **CU0** the data: the 16 config rows from the live DT and held after their clamps, 18 clamp cases on their bounds; `BlocStance` Zarya 20, Meridian 40, the CSV equal to the live DT, an injected row without the column -1.
- **CU1** inertness, E5 on against off (`Debug_SetCurrencyEnabled`) in each band, 10 sols with a Defy, a Comply and a Pacify through the uplink: the digest (stocks, shelves, both purses, relations, Influence, HumanNature, tension, identity, the multiplier, Hope, the population, the quota phase, the projected ending) bit-identical, the saves byte-identical up to `'RHE5'`, the logs identical; the on twin's record moved and logged nothing. **CU1b** the unissued multiplier equals HEAD's expression bit for bit on 20 identity x tension cases.
- **CU2** one rate step by hand on 12 fixtures against hand-written integers (the 1 mcr minimum, both clamps, truncation not floor, an anchor beyond +10000, a negative identity's remainder) and the band. **CU2b** 10,000 seeded steps against an independent integer reference in the suite: 0 differ, 0 move more than 2% or 1 mcr, 0 leave [250, 4000].
- **CU3** issuing: every refusal with its text (an injected stance-0 rival for the identity text), a refused DefyIssue leaves its Defy undone; DefyIssue against a plain Defy from one save (tension +10, the multiplier exactly 0.13 lower, two journal entries, and one alert each: D44 alone, and D44 whole with the issue's clause after it); the issue roll (issue sol s, open s + 2, 1000 mcr, no step until the roll closing s + 1); a stand-alone Issue at +20 with its own one alert.
- **CU4** joining: two rivals created in the reverse of their name order join in name order; a Greed-shaped rival (a copy of Zarya's row, built as the commandlet builds its Greed: the check is that the inject keeps the stance 20 it was given and has no economy) and a stance of -1 never join; a stance-40 rival joins at the roll after a Defy brings the record to +40, from that sol + 2; an embargoed member is refused at booking with HEAD's text and stays a member.
- **CU5** settlement: a mark order (its reservation read in marks, `MarkSpend` at the clearing, the owed marks paid at the return, the bloc tally); a converted credit order whose price and rate each move a full step between booking and clearing (the reservation covers it; the charge rounded up and the owed rounded down, to the milli-credit; the bloc tally of its clearing sol stays empty, for a charge and for an owed: only mark orders count); both purse invariants after every step.
- **CU6** the desk: both directions at 1.036 (the 300 cr pay 280.888 marks with ONE floor, where flooring twice would give 280.887; the 200 marks back 200.984 cr), the 3% kept, the reservation set and released; the cap each way (`... 500 credits ...`, `... 500 marks ...`), check 3 (`No bloc member can trade with you` with the one member embargoed) and the spendable refusal; a pure desk order leaves the next rate step unchanged; a one-sol round trip returns 0.9597 of its stake (at most 0.960).
- **CU7** the record: 30 sols with no act (the tension record is the start + 600 a sol, the live tension within 0.15 of it); a Defy 10 s past a boundary keyed to its own sol; a Comply from tension 5 clamps at 0; a 12-sol jump adds 12 drifts; no drift with no `SliceActive` row; the sync case in two orders; each act's entry against hand-written values (a Comply from a record of 65000 at identity +10: 65000 + 600 - 45000 = 20600 and the identity record -10000, the value it left; a Pacify from 50000: 30600, and the identity record unmoved by a harness shift); the clamp after EACH entry (a Pacify, then an Issue, in one sol from 5000: 10000, where one clamp at the end gives 0); a 12-sol jump with a Comply due in the second sol of its last pass (56800: 100000, -45000, three drifts; every drift first would give 55000).
- **CU8** era parity (below). **CU9** the withdrawal: the Comply raises one alert (D43) and ends the penalty at once; the market closes (an order to a discovered member with an open route refused; the desk refuses with the same text; a new issue `The mark is being withdrawn`); the withdrawal waits for every priced order, then redeems the balance and the on-road owed marks with ONE floor at 3.499 (the purse topped up so the two parts' remainders, 499 and 800 milli-credits, pass a whole one together: a floor per part would pay one milli-credit less), the convoy on the road pays nothing more, every slot re-expressed and clamped, `THE MARK IS RETIRED`, the bloc dissolved, a later issue allowed with an empty purse; a doctored wait fixture; an issue and a withdrawal at one roll at 1000 (all four entries checked: Defy +20 and -12000, Issue +10000, Comply 0 and -45000, Withdraw nothing); with the market live, twins from one save (a roll without the Comply and the same roll with it): every one of the 18 slots is the roll's reprice re-expressed at 3.499 and held in its band; the reopen edge (refused in the era band, booked in the agent band).
- **CU10** the save: `'RHE5'` straight after `'RHE4'`'s footer (found by tag) with payload `GetCurrencyBlockBytes()`, save-load-save byte-identical, the two pending acts' entries (the Defy +40000 and -12000, the Pacify -20000 and no identity); every list read back ascending (the journal and the members in the fixture; the desk orders, #63 and #66, on `cu10cut`; two mark orders, convoys #1 and #2, on `cu10two`); a v55 file refused (`/56`); 19 doctored, resealed repairs each with its own Warning and no Error (the first build's 10; the first fix round's four: both flags up, the withdrawal standing; a malformed frame, a journal count of 9999; a truncated frame, 3 bytes short; the lifetime totals' precedence; and fix round 2's five: a rate below the band (100 -> 250), the tension record below its range (-5 -> 0), a repeated journal key, a repeated member (the frame rebuilt for its length), a desk order above the daily cap dropped); E4's reserved counter recomputed with E4's text (desk orders included); a mark order whose convoy E4's reader dropped dropped silently, with the exact Warning set pinned (E4's drop and the reserved-marks counter set to the orders' 0: two Warnings, none for the order); a lost `'RHE4'` and a lost `'RHE5'` each starting the mark unissued (the record read from the identity and tension just loaded); E4's block started fresh three more ways (a slot count of -1, a slot table out of order, a region written twice), each dropping the currency frame with ruling 2's Warning and recording its reason (Malformed, SlotsReset, RegionsRepeated); a fresh process's `NextCommandId` raised to exactly one past the newest command, both where that is a journal act (`cu10`: 66) and where it is a pending desk order (`cu10cut`: 67, past the journal's #65); R4's cut order in the mark purse (a balance 50 marks short cuts the pending exchange alone; 110 short drops it and cuts the convoy by 10, the Warnings in that order); and R7 O8's exception on the two consistent files saved between an issue act and its roll - a stand-alone Issue (no Warning; still issued with the penalty and the entry; the next roll issues the market at 1000 for sol 2) and DefyIssue with a Comply in one sol (no Warning; the withdrawal pending, the four entries kept; the roll issues and withdraws at 1000, 1234.567 marks for 1234.567 cr).
- **CU11** ruling 11: the rate forced to 250, to 4000 or left at parity, and a billion marks held, for 10 sols: Hope, the population, Influence, HumanNature, the credit, the districts and every stock and shelf bit-identical. **CU12** politics: 25 sols after a plain Defy and a DefyIssue from one save differ only by the issue's +10 tension and -0.10 multiplier (the Defy's effects, relations, Influence, Hope, the population and the projected ending equal; no embargo in either).
- **CU8, era parity.** The start save `cu8start` (Kestrel at 30 km with stance 40 and relation 10 beside Zarya and Meridian, the clock at sol 16.0 exactly by the real roll); both twins sync at load and run sols 16-36 with the brief's schedule on the era grid (DefyIssue 19.5; an exchange just after the sol-20 boundary, refused in both with `The desk opens at the end of sol 21`; ToMarks 500 at 22.5; Kestrel at 23.5 converted; Zarya at 25.5 in marks, a net sale; the boundary Defy just after 26.0; Kestrel just after 27.0 converted and at 29.5 in marks, a net sale; ToCredits 200 at 28.5; `Debug_AddTension(25)` at 30.0; a Pacify at 30.5; Zarya just after 31.0 in marks; a Comply at 34.5). At all 20 thresholds the rate, both views, the members, every slot's price, every economy rival's store of every slot, every fill, every charge in either currency, every desk payout and the bloc tally are identical; each order settles as the table says; at 30.0 Earth embargoes Zarya with a third demand pending; the record does not see the +25 (53400 at the roll closing 29 and just after the write) and the roll closing 30 moves it by exactly 600 - 20000 (to 34000); the Pacify leaves the demand pending; the end balances are equal and the withdrawal at the roll closing 34 redeems the same credit in both (1,844.885 cr; Zarya's last convoy home in sol 34 in the era twin and 35 in the agent twin, as the re-review designed); R7's three rules hold at every booking (the sol-28.5 exchange: 200 marks of 504.682 held; the marks held without a convoy home within a sol read the same in both twins at all four mark bookings); no award, launch or sale lands.

- **Mutations (CU13), each built, run and restored by sha256**, all 18 re-run in fix round 2 against the final sources and the 63-check suite (a driver: apply one, build, run `-currency`, restore, touch, hash; `RHSimCurrency.cpp` `f69a225fbe911d54...` and `RHSimWorldSubsystem.cpp` `fd3c55d58e60ab5d...` before every mutation and after every restore; every mutated file, its build log, its run log and its result with the failing lines and the suite's `CURRENCY info` lines in `Saved/RHCapture/e5/gate3/mutations/`, the summary in `all_results.txt`). Existing suites updated: the version checks (55 to 56: 14 `Ver == 56`, 14 `"/56"` and 3 `(expect 56)` literals across 9 suite files and the commandlet) - the "a v54 file is refused" fixtures stay, as the brief says; XE10 finds `'RHE5'` by tag and asserts the market frame's footer meets its header (its printed text unchanged); MX1's compare is bounded to the market frame's own bytes. **-scale** (run separately, three times on the final binary): 0 failures; every digest identical to the pre-change binary's (128ac334, bfe99266, b7ea20c9, 5f9d7352, recalled b193ca3a, reloaded d6c87c14); the new line, 1,000 `RollMarket` calls with the mark issued and 8 bloc members at N = 5,000: **0.894, 0.863 and 0.855 ms** (median 0.863) against the budget of 1.260 ms, E4's line measured on the pre-change binary in the first round (0.727, 0.760 and 0.771 ms, median 0.760, + 0.5; reused as recorded: the pre-change binary is not reinstalled); E4's own line 0.767, 0.741 and 0.738 ms (its `<= 2 ms` check passes; the in-run E5 <= E4 + 0.5 check passes each time). **Playtest identity.** `RH_PLAYTEST_DISTRICTS=1 bash scripts/sim/rh_playtest.sh 110 e5_full guide full` on the final binary: **byte-identical** (`cmp`) to `Saved/baselines/ind_full_playtest.txt`, 296,489 bytes, 1,732 lines, 0 sim errors (the bot never sends `Solidarity`, `Pacify`, `Currency` or `Exchange`, so the journal stays empty, the mark is never issued and the record logs nothing).

**Click test** (`RH_CLICKTEST_RES=1440x860 bash scripts/unreal/rh_clicktest.sh e5_fix2_clickN 450`: the timeout raised to 450 s through the script's second positional argument): **93 steps** (HEAD's 92 and `currency.ISSUE`, the deviation above). On the final binary **three runs back to back, all PASS 93 of 93** (158, 156 and 160 s; no run failed or timed out in either fix round). **Known gaps** (left on purpose):
- No rate history and no currency panel beyond this minimal block: that is E8's (its reads are declared).
- The desk's preview sol, the deck's bloc line and the TRADE tooltip read `GetBookingSolNow()`; at the margin of a roll the preview may differ from what the colony books.

- A loaded or doctored mark purse large enough to overflow the redemption (`(Total / 1000) x r` past int64: about 9 x 10^10 marks at the largest rate the config clamps allow, about 2 x 10^15 at the shipped 4000 ceiling) is not bounded on load; no play path reaches it (the re-review's note; the desk's payout, which it named, is now split and its loaded orders capped).
- Balance numbers (the issue threshold, the penalty, the weights, the 2% step, the band, the 3% spread, the 500 cap, the stances) are chosen for legible arithmetic, not tuned play: awaiting the director.

None of the new wording is morale, sickness or evacuation wording; no E5 path reads or writes Hope, the population, growth or Influence (CU11, CU12).
- Not committed: the orchestrator commits.

### E5's banner re-raise: the director's answer (2026-10-27)

E5 is committed as `9fc2c6d`. With the placeholder numbers a DefyIssue nets -2 tension (the Defy -12, the issue +10). Earth raises its next demand at tension 60, so a DefyIssue answered at 62 or more raises the next demand in the same tick. Its `EARTH TRANSMISSION` alert then replaces D44 + Y10 on the one-slot banner; a plain Defy does the same from 72. The act's news is still in the deck's status line and the log.

**(a) leave it for the balance pass, and revisit it later.** No change now. The other options were (b) queue the banner's alerts so a second waits out the first's hold, and (c) delay the re-raise to the next sol. Revisit it when the tension numbers are tuned, or in E8 (the market panel) if that touches the banner. It is on the follow-up list in `docs/qa/2026-10-26/orchestrator-handoff.md`, together with the Gate-D wording point that Y10's clause says "so tension rises" while the act nets -2.

### Idle robots: the director's answer (2026-10-28)

The director asked for robots that stop standing around on the surface: a better formation, busy work in downtime, or a walk to their charging pads. Today a robot without a job stays where its last job ended, and the deck draws it walking to a rank of parking slots beside the Lander after 6 sim seconds (`rh.RobotRouting`, `ParkSlotCm`).

**(a), after E8: display only.** Idle robots will walk to their nearest charging pad and wait around it. Now and then one walks to a nearby building and plays its work animation as an inspection. Robots with no pad nearby keep the Lander rank. Nothing in RedHopeSim changes, so saves, the battery and the playtest stay identical. The options not taken were (b) real docking in the sim, which moves every robot baseline, and (c) both. The stub, with the points to settle when the design is written: `docs/qa/2026-10-28/robot-idle-design.md`. It is queued after E8 in `docs/qa/2026-10-26/orchestrator-handoff.md`.

### E6 the levers: what it settles, and where it deviated (2026-10-28, not yet committed)

Built from `docs/qa/2026-10-24/e6-levers-design.md` on HEAD `8da8c2a` (save v56), in the order of authority the queue set: the DIRECTOR ANSWER (**Q-A (a):** Earth cannot embargo a live pact partner; R8, the lift, is not built; **Q-B (a):** a sabotage stops the target's production for the six whole sols after the act, `SabotageCutPct` 100; **Q-C (a):** only a betrayed partner charges a tariff: 2,500 bp, every resource, both ways, 30 sols) > `docs/qa/2026-10-26/preflight/ORCHESTRATOR-RULINGS.md` (ruling 1: no gate asserts its frame is the last; ruling 6: the wording prefix Z; ruling 7: `git grep -P`; ruling 8: v57) > `CROSS-CHECK.md` section 6 (items 1, 2, 6, 7, 9, 15, 16, 17) > `E6-brief.md` > the design's REFRESH, REVISION, re-review, review and body. The brief's anchors were at `d842dd0`; E5 moved H, M, W, K and the click test, and every reference was re-anchored by symbol (H: E4's politics hooks 1617-1621 -> 1683-1687, the four sets 3947 / 3976 / 3988-3989 -> 4164 / 4193 / 4205-4206; M: `RollMarket` 509 -> 516, `MarketProduceAndReprice` 560 -> 586, `ClearConvoy` 639 -> 665, `GetOrderReservationMcr` 826 -> 901, `PlanConvoy` 851 -> 926, `CanPlaceOrder` 1027 -> 1125, `BookConvoy` 1041 -> 1140, the goodwill check 1236 -> 1351; W: the covert dispatch 9585 / 9595 / 9605 -> 9631 / 9641 / 9651, `ResolveCovert` 9795 -> 9841, `ResolveLaunder` 9865 -> 9911, `ResolveSabotage` 9904 -> 9950, `StepEarth` -> 10010, `StepPreemptive` 10081 -> 10131, `ResolvePacify` 10151 -> 10201, `ResolveDilemma` 10347 -> 10399, the v21 / v22 writes 13708-13726 -> 13776-13782, the tail after `'RHE5'` 13928-13933 -> 13991, the mid-file reads 14361-14385 -> 14433-14441; K: the rival card 2355 -> 2399, `HandleOrderNudge` 2646 -> 3025; V 4249-4251 -> 4977-4979; the click test's market step 1754 -> 1890). E5 as built, against the brief's A1-A10: the `'RHE5'` frame is written at W:13991 and read straight after `ReadMarketBlock`, then `ReconcileReservations()` (A1); the journal is `FRHPoliticalEntry` with `ERHPoliticalAct { Defy, Comply, Pacify, Issue, Withdraw }`, to which E6 appends `PactSign` and `PactBreak` (A2); `ResolveDilemma` and `ResolvePacify` take the command, the three covert resolvers did not, and E6 gives them `const FRHCommand&` (A3); `CanExchange`'s check 3 read the three sets directly (RHSimCurrency.cpp:506), and so did `CanIssueCurrency` (:381), a reader the brief's table does not name (T8): both now read `IsRouteClosed` / `HasDefected` / `IsEmbargoed` (A4); the mark as the brief describes (A5); `PlanConvoy` already takes E5's clearing sol, reused as EffSol (A6); `BlocStance`, `Debug_SyncPoliticalView()`, `GetCurrencyBlockBytes()` and CU8's harness exist (A7); `bMarketBlockLost` exists and is not needed (A8); the baseline is `Saved/baselines/battery_post_e5` (59 runs) (A9); MX1's compare is bounded to the market frame (A10). After E6, `git grep -n -P '\b(ClosedRoutes|SabotageSols|EmbargoGrace|DefectedRivals)\b' Source` is empty (the load's temporaries are named `Mirror*`; `GetEmbargoGrace` and the config's `EmbargoGraceSols` keep their names).

**What E6 builds.** (Every number below is a placeholder awaiting the director.)
- **One list of trade rules** (`TArray<FRHTradeRule> TradeRules`, ascending by Id; `NextRuleId` saved, from 1, never reused) replaces HEAD's `ClosedRoutes`, `SabotageSols`, `EmbargoGrace` and `DefectedRivals`. Kinds `Closed` 1, `Disrupted` 2, `Severed` 3, `Embargo` 4 (the refusal precedence), `Pact` 5, `Tariff` 6; origins `Comply` 1, `Sabotage` 2, `Defection` 3, `EarthPressure` 4, `Signed` 5, `Betrayal` 6, `Harness` 7. Windows are half-open `[From, Until)`: none `(-1, -1)`, closed, or open-ended (`Until -1`); each kind has its legal shapes (D8) and its exclusivity (D9). A repeated sabotage **appends** a rule. One walk, `EvaluateTradeRules(Rival, EffSol, Lines)`, gives the block (the lowest kind among the live blocking rules, then the lowest Id, with HEAD's text for that kind), each line's markup (the sum of the covering tariffs, clamped to `TariffMaxBp`) and whether a pact window covers EffSol; the market never reads a live timer, a live flag or an Id. `PlanConvoy` (so `Convoy`, `MarketOrder`, `CanPlaceOrder`, `CanPlaceStandingOrder`) and `CanSignPact` take the walk's block; E5's desk reads only `Closed`, `Severed` and `Embargo` (sabotage never refuses the desk, R7). The countdowns are HEAD's arithmetic statement for statement (the same `DtSols`, the same function and point, tested `<= 0.0`): `StepEarth` counts each live `Disrupted` rule down in Id order and raises HEAD's `RECOVERED` alert when a rival's last one ends; `StepPreemptive` counts each `Embargo` down and hardens it into a `Severed` rule in place (its Id kept, origin `Defection`). Retirement runs at the very end of `RollMarket` (D10 with re-review O2). The goodwill check reads the list (`Severed`, `Embargo`, `Closed`).
- **Pacts.** A new uplink verb `Treaty` (`Target` the neighbour, `Text` `Sign` or `Break`, any case). `CanSignPact` refuses in D16's order with its texts (Z5). Signing, at execution: Influence -15, Earth tension +5 (journaled +5,000, so the mark's rate reads it at the sol's close), HumanNature +3, a live `Pact` rule `[c + 2, open)`, one alert and the log line. A pact whose window covers an order's EffSol raises that order's cargo cap from `ConvoyCargoKg` (1,000) to `PactCargoKg` (2,000) each way (a barter neighbour's lots are fixed). A Break, at execution: the pact's live part ends and its window closes at `max(c + 2, From)`; relation -25, HumanNature -10, tension -5 (journaled -5,000); the betrayal tariff (Q-C a): 2,500 bp on every resource both ways, `[c + 2, c + 32)`, origin `Betrayal` (never above `TariffMaxBp`: the config's one cross-row rule, `ClampBetrayalTariffToMax` in `InitLevers`, and a clamp where the rule is made, so no load repair ever rewrites it). A Comply that closes a partner's route ends the pact with no tariff (`=== PACT ENDED ... ===`); a caught covert op or sabotage against a partner ends it from the partner's side with the betrayal tariff and no further relation or HumanNature change (`=== PACT BROKEN by %s: they caught you ===`); a clean op leaves it standing. **Q-A (a):** `StepPreemptive`'s target filter skips a rival with a live pact, so Earth embargoes the next eligible neighbour or nobody; nothing lifts an embargo (R8 not built).
- **Tariffs, integer only.** `tariff = ceil(value x m / 10000)`, a buy costs `value + tariff`, a sell receives `value - tariff` (`MarketTariffMcr`, `MarketLineSettleMcr`); with `m = 0` every figure is E4's and E5's to the milli-unit. The reservation is `HiM = Hi + ceil(Hi x m)`, `LoM = max(0, Lo - ceil(Lo x m))`, then E5's `HiR` / `LoR` for a credit order after the issue. `ValueMcr` (the goodwill floor), E4's tally and E5's bloc tally stay at the clearing price before the markup. `TariffPaidMcr` counts the orders cleared before the market counts in marks, `TariffPaidMmk` those cleared while it does (in the market's unit). No markup is negative and the desk carries none. The clearing charges the markup recomputed at the stored EffSol (`max(ClearSol, MarketCheckedSol)` at booking, kept per uncleared priced convoy in E6's own payload, never in `'RHE4'`); the booking markups are stored only so LV8 can assert the two are equal (T3).
- **Covert acts that move the market.** A sabotage's new `Disrupted` rule gets the market window `[c + 1, c + 1 + SabotageMarketSols)` (6): at every roll closing a sol inside a rival's windows its production is cut by `SabotageCutPct` (100, Q-B a) in both of E4's uses (the store, and step 3's Prod term), computed locally (T2: `ProdSumG` and `ProduceG` are never written), consumption going on; the live refusal is HEAD's (6 sols on the timer), caught or not. A clean `Covert` against a rival with an economy records one pending `Theft` per looted resource, and a `Launder` one pending `Offering` per slot it spent, keyed by the act's own sol, `CommandId` and slot order; a caught op and a barter rival record nothing. The roll applies every effect due, in `(ClearSol, CommandId, Sub)` order, after the convoy and desk clearing and the ring advance, before production and outside the live gate (a theft takes `min(grams, store)`); neither kind enters a tally. The covert roll is untouched (no new `RollCovertDetected`, `CovertAttempts` and the roll's inputs as HEAD's).
- **The switch.** `bLeversEnabled` (runtime only, default on, `Debug_SetLeversEnabled`): off, `Treaty` is refused, no effect is recorded, a `Disrupted` rule gets no window and every markup reads 0; the roll reads it too (3.3: `RollMarket`'s cut windows and `GetRivalCutPct`), so a window made while the levers were on cuts nothing while they are off (LV6). LV1 (d) reads each of the four gates directly (a later gate can hide an earlier one: a window made with the levers off would still cut nothing, since the roll reads the switch too). The migration is not switchable. E4's five politics hooks are rebuilt on the list with origin `Harness` (D30), so MX11 does not move.
- **Save v57.** E6's payload sits in its own frame `'RHL6'`, written straight after E5's `'RHE5'` and read after E5's reader (and after `ReconcileReservations`): FormatRev 1, `NextRuleId`, the rules by Id (names as strings; `*` for every resource), the pending effects, the two lifetime tariff totals, and the terms of every uncleared priced convoy (EffSol and one booking markup per line). A default colony's block is 33 bytes, so every save grows by 53 (12 + 33 + 8). The v19, v21 and v22 slots stay, byte for byte in HEAD's layout, as a mirror projected from the rules (D31); a lost or malformed block rebuilds the rules from the mirror (D33: `Closed` by name string, then `Severed`, `Embargo`, and `Disrupted` with no window), with one Warning naming what is lost. Every other repair is a Warning and the colony always loads (Z8); no load path logs an Error.
- **Presentation (minimal; E8 is the full panel).** On each neighbour's card: the pact row (`Sign a pact (15 Influence)` with `SIGN`, greyed by `CanSignPact` with the reason on a dim line under the row, `Cannot sign a pact now: %s.`, and in its tooltip, as E5's issue button shows its own; or, with a live pact, `Pact since sol %d` - the sol it was signed in, `GetLivePactSignedSol`, always one already reached - with ` (bigger convoys from sol %d)` while the pact's window is still ahead of the market's open sol, and `BREAK`); the tooltips name every cost and when each term starts (SIGN: the Influence, the tension and the conduct shift +3, Earth's protection once it lands, the colony's bigger convoys to them from two sols after; BREAK: the standing -25, the conduct shift -10, the tension -5, and the tariff from two sols after it lands); the amber tariff line (`Tariff %s until sol %d`) and the disruption line (`Production stopped until sol %d`), each naming its first sol too while its window has not opened (`Tariff 25% from sol 2 until sol 31`), and the disruption line's word following the config's cut (`stopped` at `SabotageCutPct` 100, `cut N%` otherwise, no line at 0) (Z1-Z3); a percentage is printed exactly (`FormatPercentBp`: 2500 `25%`, 2550 `25.5%`), never `Bp / 100` truncated, here and in `PACT BROKEN`'s alert and log line; the order's name `Pact`, and on the uplink list `Pact <neighbour> (sign)` / `(break)` (Z4); the order draft's per-line clamp is the sim's own cap for an order sent now (`GetOrderCargoCapKg`: `PactCargoKg` when a pact's window covers the sol that order would clear in, else `ConvoyCargoKg`; the brief's P7). Console `RH.Treaty <Rival> sign|break` and `RH.Levers` (Z9; `, ended` follows a tariff only once its window is behind the open sol).

**UNCERTAIN items of the brief, each with its stated default taken.**
- **T1, "every resource" in the save:** the sentinel `*` (never `""`, which `RHSaveFrame::ReadName` refuses), pinned in LV9.
- **T6, displayed end sols:** a half-open window names its last sol, `Until - 1`, everywhere a player or a log reads it (the tariff line, the disruption line, `PACT BROKEN`'s "from sol %d to sol %d", `CanSignPact`'s "(until sol %d)").
- **Section 3.4, the rows the spec gives no clamp:** every one of the 13 rows is clamped (`LeversConfigClamp`, one table for the sim and LV0): the relation, tension and HumanNature rows 0..100, `PactInfluenceCost` 0..1000, `PactCargoKg` 1..10,000 (E5's rate step multiplies the 8-sol bloc tally by 10^4: its int64 bound, `8 x 3 x cap x 4 x 900,000 x 10^4`, holds up to about 10,700 kg; the first build's 1..1,000,000 did not), `PactBetrayalTariffBp` and `TariffMaxBp` 0..9999 (so a sale can never turn into a charge), with the one cross-row rule `PactBetrayalTariffBp <= TariffMaxBp`, `PactBetrayalTariffSols` 1..1000, `SabotageMarketSols` 1..30, `SabotageCutPct` 0..100; LV0 probes 26 values against them, pins the cross-row rule, and runs `InitLevers` itself on all 13 rows out of range (each held clamped, the betrayal tariff at the cap).
- **Section 8, a pact step in the click test:** added, as recommended (the deviation below).
- **Section 7, a `-currency` line that prints an unmasked whole-file size:** none moved (the battery below).
- **A6:** E5's `PlanConvoy` already takes the clearing sol (its `EffSol` parameter), so E6 reuses it and adds none.

**Deviations** (each kept as small as the gate allowed):
- **The effect's `CommandId` is saved as an int32**, not widened to int64 as the brief's section 4 lays it out: `CROSS-CHECK.md` item 9 (which outranks the brief) allows matching E5's journal, and `FRHCommand` holds an int32. The effect is 4 bytes shorter.
- **A stored EffSol that is not `max(ClearSol, MarketCheckedSol)` is re-derived from the convoy silently**, not with repair 11's Warning, whichever side it falls on (the first two builds re-derived only one below the clearing sol; one past it - only a resealed file holds one - would have made the clearing read markups at a sol its reservation was not booked for). An uncleared convoy's EffSol is exactly that value (booked at `max(ClearSol, the open sol then)`, and no roll has closed its clearing sol since, or it would have cleared), so a consistent save never meets the repair. **Why silent, exactly:** E4's block is authoritative for the convoy and accepts a moved clearing sol without a word, and E5's CU9 moves one on purpose and asserts that the load logs no Warning at all (`RHSimCurrencySuite.cpp:1565`, `bQuiet`): a Warning here does not just add a line to `currency.log` (cross-check item 16), it **fails CU9** (the first build's Warning did: `quick1/currency.log`, "CURRENCY FAIL: CU9 ... [first failing condition: #1]"). LV9 asserts the silence on both sides (`lv9c2` EffSol - 3, `lv9c6` EffSol + 3; mutation 30). A terms entry whose convoy E4's reader dropped, or whose market block was lost, is dropped silently too (ruling 1 (2), cross-check item 2); the Warning stays for a terms entry whose convoy loaded and disagrees in its line count, or has cleared. **For the orchestrator:** cross-check item 2 ("keep the Warning only for a terms entry whose convoy loaded but disagrees (LineCount, EffSol)") cannot hold its EffSol half once E5's CU9 exists; ruling 1 (2) adopted item 2 so that `market.log` and `currency.log` gain no Warnings, and item 16 requires `currency.log` line-identical. This build keeps the LineCount Warning and makes the EffSol repair silent. Accept that in writing at commit, or amend item 2 to drop "EffSol", so D6e, D7 or E8 does not restore the Warning and turn CU9 red.
- **A `Tariff` rule carries `bLive` false and a non-tariff rule `Dir` 3**: neither field means anything for that kind, so each is made with one fixed value (the loader takes them as read: they change nothing).
- **Load repairs beyond section 4's list**, each a Warning and each exercised in LV9: a markup on any kind but a tariff is set to 0 (only a tariff carries one; `lv9r9`, a closed route's 500 bp); a live timer on a kind that has none (`Closed`, `Severed`, `Pact`, `Tariff`) is set to 0 (`lv9t7`, a pact's 3 sols); a disruption whose window the upper clamp empties keeps no window and its live part (`lv9w11`), and a tariff it empties is dropped (`lv9w10`).
- **Four small load and clearing hardenings the review asked for** (none moves a gate line): a load raises `NextCommandId` past every pending effect's `CommandId` (E5's trap 7 extended: a fresh process's next act never takes a pending one's Id; LV9); a `NextRuleId` below `max(1, the last rule's Id + 1)` or above 2^30 makes the block malformed, the rules then rebuilt from the mirror (repair 1, now also for a frame with no rule; LV9); an embargo beside a defection is dropped by a `RemoveAll` whose predicate reads a set gathered first, never the array it compacts; and `ClearConvoy`'s no-terms case (no path makes one) reads `max(ClearSol, the closing sol)`, the loader's default, with its booking markups left empty, so a test comparing the booking and clearing markups can never compare a verdict with itself.
- **The load Warnings name a kind with its article** (`an embargo`, `a pact`); the first build printed `a embargo`.
- **SIGN's reason is also printed under the pact row** (`Cannot sign a pact now: %s.`, dim, while `CanSignPact` refuses), not only in its tooltip as the brief has it: E4's SEND ORDER and E5's DEFY, AND ISSUE and desk SEND all show a greyed button's reason on a visible line, and the first build's card read as if Influence were the gate when the relation was (the review's finding). It costs the card two lines while a pact cannot be signed.
- **`Pact since sol N` prints the sol the pact was signed in** (`GetLivePactSignedSol` = the window's first sol - 2). The spec gives the text but never defines N; the first build printed the window's first sol, a sol still ahead for two sols after a signing. While that window is still ahead of the market's open sol the row adds ` (bigger convoys from sol %d)`.
- **The card names a term's first sol while it is still ahead** (`Tariff 25% from sol 2 until sol 31`, `Production stopped from sol 1 until sol 6`, the pact's `(bigger convoys from sol 2)`), beyond the spec's texts (`25% until sol 91`, `production stopped until sol 66`), so a term the act has scheduled never reads as one in force (design risk 1: both clocks legible). Once the window covers the market's open sol the line is the spec's.
- **The disruption line's word follows `SabotageCutPct`**: `Production stopped` at 100 (the authored value, Q-B a), `Production cut %d%%` at any other value, no line at 0 or with the harness's levers off. The spec's text assumes 100.
- **Percentages are printed exactly** (`FormatPercentBp`: 2500 `25%`, 2550 `25.5%`, 2505 `25.05%`), on the card, in BREAK's tooltip, and in `PACT BROKEN`'s alert and log line, where the brief's D19 has `they charge %d%%` (`Bp / 100`, truncated). The default config prints the same text either way.
- **The order draft's clamp is the sim's cap for an order sent now** (`GetOrderCargoCapKg`: the walk's pact flag at `GetBookingSolNow()`, the sol PlanConvoy itself reads), not `HasLivePact`, which the brief's section 3.9 parenthetical names. The live flag opens two sols before the window and closes two sols before it ends (`EndPact` keeps `[From, c + 2)`), so the deck held drafts to 2,000 kg two sols early and to 1,000 kg for the two sols an ended pact still carries 2,000; the walk's flag closes both edges.
- **The signing's alert is headed `PACT SIGNED`** (the first build's `A PACT`), mirroring `PACT BROKEN` and keeping the corrected sentence on one banner line at 1440x860 (Z6; placeholder wording).
- **The mirror rebuild's origins:** a rule rebuilt from the mid-file record takes the origin its kind is made with in play (`Closed` from `Comply`, `Severed` from `Defection`, `Embargo` from `EarthPressure`, `Disrupted` from `Sabotage`); an embargo beside a rebuilt defection is skipped (D9).
- **A party name differing only in case** is read as its row with a Warning (repair 3). UE's `FString ==` ignores case, so the loader compares with `ESearchCase::CaseSensitive`; the first build did not, and LV9's case-only fixture caught it.
- **Transient records are trimmed:** the effects as applied and the production cuts (for tests and the deck) keep the newest 64, trimmed whenever they pass 128 (amortised, so a roll never shifts the array) and keeping their capacity (`EAllowShrinking::No`, as the pending effects' queue does when it applies one), and `RollMarket` walks the rules once per pass for both the cut windows and whether anything can retire (it retires only then). This round also moved the retirement test, `TradeRuleRetiresAt`, into the header as an inline function, body unchanged: under the adaptive build the modified `RHSimMarket.cpp` and `RHSimLevers.cpp` are separate translation units, so `RollMarket`'s walk made one out-of-line call per rule per roll (60 in `-scale`), and inlining it took the E6 line from 1.080 / 1.110 / 1.070 ms to 0.958 / 0.962 / 1.027 on otherwise identical sources, every digest identical. All are for `-scale` (below); none is saved.
- **More hooks and getters than the brief names**, all for the tests and the deck: `Debug_EnqueueAt` (an uplink order at a given execution time, appended, to build the era band's queue inversion), `Debug_SetRelation`, `Debug_RemoveRule`, `Debug_AddTradeRule`, `Debug_AddMarketEffect`, `GetLedgerSlotIndex`, `GetCovertAttempts`, `GetMarketEffectsApplied`, `GetProductionCuts`, `GetDisruptionUntilSol`, `GetDisruptionWindow` (the windows still ahead as one span: they never leave a gap), `GetLivePactFromSol`, `GetLivePactSignedSol`, `GetBetrayalTariff` (with its first sol, optional), `GetOrderCargoCapKg` (the draft's clamp), `GetSabotageCutPct`, `IsLeversBlockLost`, `GetLeversConfig`, `LeversConfigClamp`, `FormatPercentBp`, `Debug_SetTariffConfig` (LV0: the two tariff rows through their clamps and the cross-row rule) and `Debug_InitLeversWith` (LV0: `InitLevers` itself with rows given in place of the config's, through the same body, `InitLeversFrom`); the cost getters for the deck's tooltips (`GetPactHumanNatureShift` and `GetPactBreakHumanNatureShift` among them). Each `Debug_` hook is harness-only, as HEAD's.
- **One click step the brief does not name** (it left it UNCERTAIN): `pact.setup` raises Zarya's relation to 60 and tops Influence up to the pact's cost + 5 (the harness, as the market step's setup does), then `pact.SIGN` clicks SIGN on Zarya's card and asserts a `Treaty Sign` to Zarya is on the uplink. The click test is 94 steps.
- **One QA cheat the brief does not name:** `RH.Levers trust <Rival>` sets that neighbour's relation to 60 and tops Influence up to the pact's cost + 5 (DEBUG/QA, for captures, like E5's `RH.Currency grant`).
- **`RH.Treaty` warns and sends anyway** when `CanSignPact` / `CanBreakPact` would refuse (the worker judges it when it lands), as `RH.Solidarity` does.
- **The battery's log directory.** The first gate ran the battery as `RH_BATTERY_LOGDIR=Saved/RHCapture/e6/battery bash scripts/sim/rh_battery.sh 10`, which left E5's 59 logs in `Saved/RHBattery` (so the brief's freshness test and diff read E5's logs, and a `battery_post_e6` snapshot taken from there would have frozen them). This gate runs it with the default directory: `Saved/RHBattery` holds the E6 battery on the final binary, and `Saved/RHCapture/e6/battery` is a time-preserving copy of it (`diff -r` empty). Snapshot `Saved/baselines/battery_post_e6` from `Saved/RHBattery`.

**Test-side choices.**
- **LV2 replays the four political suites' scripts in full** (`-covert`, `-espionage`, `-preemptive`, `-solidarity`), each in a fresh colony in the era band, with their save round trips. **LV1 runs one composite script of their acts, not the four scripts** (a deviation from the brief's LV1 row): in both bands, after 10 sols of standing trade, two covert ops, a launder, one sabotage and a convoy it refuses, an embargo (tension +75) and a convoy it refuses, Pacify, a demand (tension +65) answered with a Comply, and a convoy the closed route refuses, from T0 + 17.4 to 29.9 sols, past `Storm_1` (sols 12-15), whose held convoy would otherwise turn a refusal check into a storm wait; it leaves out `-covert`'s third and fourth ops, `-espionage`'s second (caught) sabotage, `-preemptive`'s re-embargo and defection and `-solidarity`'s two Defy answers. In both bands it holds a theft, an offering (the launder, after the two covert ops leave a grievance), the six cut rolls and every refusal kind; it holds no `Treaty` and no tariff, and the sabotage-window gate is hidden there behind the roll's own switch read (an off-twin window would still cut nothing), so **LV1 (d) reads each of D29's four gates directly** (the markups at 0 and an untaxed clearing, no theft and no offering recorded, a sabotage rule with no window, a Sign and a Break refused). A third pair of twins with no covert op (so no theft, no sabotage and no offering: its launder is refused in both, `No hidden grievance with Zarya Station to defuse`, and asserted so) holds the credits and prices identical too. **For the orchestrator:** accept this deviation in writing at commit, or have LV1 widened to the four suites' full scripts in both bands. The `-icelow` / `-arrivals` drift timeline (an embargo after 2,500 steps of drift, the defection 161 steps later) is replayed by its schedule, not by running those suites (each takes minutes); the suites themselves run in the battery and stay line-identical.
- **LV4 builds its tariffs with `Debug_AddTariff`** (so the arithmetic checks are independent of the pact timing that LV5 and LV8 check); its second window is `[Eff, Eff + 1)`, never negative.
- **LV8's start** (`lv8start`: Kestrel, Meridian and Zarya with economies, the clock at sol 16.0) is CU8's; Kestrel's `ExportLot` is set to `Ice:150` in the fixture so a clean covert op has something to steal. The paired run takes 20 roll thresholds; its covert rolls are clean whatever P (Zarya at attempt 0, U 0.907; Kestrel at attempt 1, U 0.727).
- **Sizes are printed only in the masked `(N bytes` form** (cross-check item 15), so `levers.log` will not move at a later gate's size change.
- **The cleared-convoy terms case has a fixture of its own** (`lv9c7`): LV9's main fixture holds no cleared priced convoy at its save, so an order to Zarya is booked, its clearing rolled and the colony saved before the convoy is home, and a terms entry naming it is added to that save's block with `LevSuiteReframe` (every doctored offset is parsed from the file, so nothing else moves). A consistent save never writes such an entry; the loader drops it with its Warning.

**Tests.** New `-levers` suite (`RHSimLeversSuite.cpp`, in `rh_battery.sh` after `-currency`: 59 suites, 60 runs), **46 checks** (the base save 1, LV0 4, LV1 4, LV2 5, LV3 5, LV4 5, LV5 7, LV6 2, LV7 1, LV8 2, LV9 8, LV10 1, LV11 1), each with its conditions tried in order so a failure names the first that failed (`[first failing condition: #n]`); no path logs an Error on purpose:
- **LV0** the 13 rows read their authored values from the live DT (13 of 13), the sim holds them, the CSV equals the DT, and `SabotageMarketSols` equals `round(SabotageDurationSols)`; each row's clamp holds on 26 probes (`PactCargoKg` 1..10,000); with `TariffMaxBp` 2000 below `PactBetrayalTariffBp` 2500 the sim holds 2000, a Break's tariff is made at 2000 bp, its texts say 20%, and save, load (no Warning) and save are byte-identical; `FormatPercentBp` prints 2500, 2550, 2505, 9000, 1 and 0 bp exactly; and `InitLevers` itself (through `Debug_InitLeversWith`, the same body) reads all 13 rows out of range or unrounded - `TariffMaxBp` 1500.4 under `PactBetrayalTariffBp` 12000 - into their clamped values (13 of 13: the betrayal tariff held at the cap, 1500; the cargo cap 10,000; the cut 50 from 49.6), then a plain `InitLevers` reads the authored config back (13 of 13), so neither the per-row clamps in `InitLevers` nor its cross-row call can be dropped unseen (mutations 25 and 26).
- **LV1** one composite script of the four political suites' acts (above) after 10 sols of standing trade, 30.5 sols, in the era band and in the agent band: the digest (relations, hidden tension, Influence, HumanNature, identity, tension, the refusals, the timers, `CovertAttempts`, every stock, Hope, the quota phase, E5's record and journal) is bit-identical with the levers on and off, every refusal is HEAD's text, the saves are byte-identical up to E1's frame (the v19 / v21 / v22 mirror included), and only the on twin's theft and six cut rolls moved Zarya's stores and prices; a third pair with no covert op (its launder refused in both, asserted) is identical in the digest, the prices and the credits; and **(d) D29 gate by gate**, with the levers off: a 2,500 bp buy tariff and a 9,000 bp sell tariff made while they were on read 0 (2,500 and 9,000 with them on), and `Ice:333;Struct:-7` books with no markup in its terms and clears untaxed at net 964.326 cr (LV4's untaxed net), tariff 0; a clean op and a launder against Zarya record no effect; a sabotage's rule has no window (`GetDisruptionUntilSol` -1) while its live refusal and 6-sol timer are HEAD's, and the next two rolls cut nothing; with a pact signed while the levers were on, `CanSignPact`, `CanBreakPact` and a sent Sign and Break are all refused with `No treaties: disabled`, the pact standing (mutations 27, 28 and 29).
