**Findings.**
- **UE's `FString ==` ignores case.** The first build's case-only party check compared names with `!=` and never warned; LV9's case-only fixture caught it. The loader and the suite now compare with `ESearchCase::CaseSensitive`.
- **`-scale` was over budget on the first build:** 1.203 ms against the same run's E5 line 0.876 + 0.2 (then 1.157 and 1.168 against 0.933 and 0.942). Three changes, none of which moves a result: the transient records are trimmed in amortised steps (no shift of the whole array at every roll), a 100% cut skips the multiply, and `RollMarket` walks the rules once a pass for the cut windows and for whether anything can retire. Then 1.097 / 1.033 / 1.146 against 0.930 / 0.884 / 0.959, and 1.035 / 1.061 / 1.029 against 0.909 / 0.905 / 0.900 on the build the mutations started from.
- **The first build warned when a stored EffSol fell below its convoy's clearing sol.** E5's CU9 moves a convoy's clearing sol on purpose (E4 accepts it silently) and asserts that the load logs no Warning at all (`RHSimCurrencySuite.cpp:1565`, `bQuiet`), so the Warning did not just add an unlisted line to `currency.log`: it failed CU9 (`quick1/currency.log`: `LoadColony v57: the terms of convoy #1 clear in sol 2, before its clearing sol 3; raised to 3`, then `CURRENCY FAIL: CU9 ... [first failing condition: #1]`). The re-derivation is silent (the deviation above), now on both sides, and LV9 asserts it.
- **A restored mutation must be newer than its object** (E5's finding): the LV12 driver restores each source by copy with a fresh file time and checks its sha256, so UBT recompiles it; the final binary was built from the restored sources, `-DisableAdaptiveUnity` first.
- **The second review's blocking findings, and what they were** (fixed in the second round): (1) LV6's second sabotage ran after `RollNext` alone, which rolls the market but never steps `StepEarth`, so the first rule's timer was still 6 when the second landed and neither the max in `GetSabotageRemaining` nor the single `RECOVERED` at the last overlapping rule's end was exercised anywhere in `-levers`; with real era steps both are pinned now (mutations 19 and 20 turn them red). (2) The first gate's battery ran with `RH_BATTERY_LOGDIR` redirected, so `Saved/RHBattery` still held E5's 59 logs: the brief's freshness test and its diff read E5's logs (that diff compared the base with itself, TOTAL 0), and a `battery_post_e6` snapshot taken the handoff's way would have frozen E5's logs into D6e's base. This gate's battery ran in the default directory.
- **`-scale`'s E6 margin was thin, and the trims cost a reallocation.** On this gate's first final build (`dcd06cff...`), six `-scale` runs at a load average of 4-7 put the E6 line 0.18, 0.25, 0.19, -0.17 (E5's line spiked), 0.17 and 0.15 ms above the same run's E5 line: run 2 failed the E6 budget (1.239 ms against 0.991 + 0.2); runs 1, 3, 4 and 5 also missed HEAD's own wall-clock budgets (run 1 both cold commutes, 12.764 and 10.920 ms against 10; run 3 the cold deep net, 3.9985 against 3; run 4 a cold commute, 11.925; run 5 the cold deep net, 4.6208), the same budgets HEAD's binary misses under load (above); run 6 was clean. The production-cut record, trimmed back to 64 every eight rolls with every rival cut, also shrank its allocation each time and grew it back, two or three reallocations per trim; trimming without shrinking took the gap to 0.12, 0.16 and 0.11 ms at the same load, and the gate below was re-run from the build on that change (the twelve mutations of `RHSimMarket.cpp` and `RHSimLevers.cpp` included). The superseded runs' logs are kept in the session scratchpad (`gate2a_superseded/`).
- **`PactCargoKg`'s first clamp (1..1,000,000) broke E5's int64 bound** on the rate step's bloc-tally product above about 10,700 kg a convoy (signed overflow); it is 1..10,000 now. E4's `ConvoyCargoKg` has no upper clamp of its own (below).
- **The first gate's mutation 4 was never seen failing its named per-order sub-check** (LV8's check stops at its first failing condition, #2); LV8 now prints its per-order sub-checks on an info line whatever the outcome, and under mutation 4 the era twin's booking == clearing comparison is red there.
- **The third review's blocking finding: LV7's "a barter rival records nothing" could not fail.** Its covert op ran by day at the injected rival's relation 30, so P was 0.425 against its U of 0.422 at attempt 2 (the roll is deterministic: the same in every run), and the op was caught every time; a caught op never reaches `RecordMarketEffects`, so the assertion held only because a caught op records nothing (already condition #2), and no check sent a Launder to a barter rival either. The op now runs at relation 60 (P 0.35: clean, its `COVERT CLEAN` line asserted) and a Launder follows it; mutation 24, which drops both of the recorder's barter guards, turns LV7 red (`leaves 1 pending effects, its launder 2`).
- **Three of D29's four gates had no check that could see them** (the review's finding): no levers-off state ever held a tariff, so the markup gate in `EvaluateTradeRules` could be deleted unseen; the sabotage-window gate was hidden behind the roll's own switch read (an off-twin window still cuts nothing); and `CanBreakPact`'s disabled refusal was never reached. LV1 (d) now reads each gate directly, and mutations 27-29 turn it red.
- **Repair 11 re-derived only an EffSol below the convoy's clearing sol**, so a resealed file with an EffSol past `max(ClearSol, the open sol)` kept it, and the clearing could charge a markup its reservation never covered; any EffSol that is not that value is re-derived now (silently, as before; mutation 30). **`InitLevers`' clamps and its cross-row call were never run on out-of-range rows** (LV0 probed the clamp table directly); `Debug_InitLeversWith` runs `InitLevers`' own body (mutations 25 and 26). **LV9 left repair 8's second defection and second embargo, the cleared-convoy terms branch, the builder's own extra repairs and D33's rebuild order unchecked**; each is checked now (mutations 31 and 32 for the kind list and the order).
- **This round's first final build missed the `-scale` budget, and the cause was an out-of-line call per rule.** Its gate (part A) ran the `-scale` A/B first: HEAD's binary gave E5's line 0.875 / 0.871 / 0.883 ms, the final binary's E6 line 1.080 / 1.110 / 1.070 (median 1.080, over both 1.063 and 1.075), and its second run failed its own in-run check (`SCALE FAIL: ... 1.110 ms (<= E5's 0.881 + 0.2)`). That gate was stopped there (the battery it had started was killed and is not reported). Under the adaptive build the modified `RHSimMarket.cpp` and `RHSimLevers.cpp` are separate translation units (`[Adaptive Build] Excluded from RedHopeSim unity file: ...`), so `RollMarket`'s one walk of the rules called `TradeRuleRetiresAt`, defined in `RHSimLevers.cpp`, out of line for every rule at every roll; moved inline into the header, body unchanged, the E6 line fell to 0.958 / 0.962 / 1.027 with the same 13 digests, and the whole gate below was run again from that build, the mutations included. **D10's retirement itself had no check** (no `-levers` check read which rules retire, so a broken retirement test would have passed every suite but `-scale`'s rule count); LV3 (d) reads it roll by roll now, and mutations 33 and 34 turn it red.

**Known gaps** (left on purpose):
- No panel beyond the card rows: E8 is the full panel (the rules, the tariffs paid, the effects).
- The banner keeps only the last alert, as before E6: a pact act's alert and another alert in the same step replace each other.
- **A partner that catches you** ends the pact and starts the 25% tariff for 30 sols with a log line only (the design's 2.1); HEAD's caught alert stays alone on the banner, and HEAD's COVERT and SABOTAGE tooltips do not warn of it (nor of Q-B's six-sol production stop). Flagged in Z6 for the director and E8's panel brief.
- **E4's `ConvoyCargoKg` has no upper clamp** (pre-existing, not an E6 row): a config above about 10,700 kg would break E5's int64 rate bound as `PactCargoKg`'s first clamp did. Left for whoever next touches E4's config.
- **The enabled SIGN tooltip and BREAK's tooltip are not captured**, and they are the two longest new strings, so their wrapping is unverified. A capture run tried (`RH.Hover SIGN`, then `RH.Hover BREAK`, each with a snapshot three seconds later): the click harness's pointer reached each button (`RHClick: 'SIGN' at (1627,594) leaf=STextBlock... reached=YES (hover only)`, and the same for BREAK), but Slate opened no tooltip in that run, and the cause was not found (the frames and log are kept in the session scratchpad, `r3/preview_r3/` and `r3/e6_capture3_preview.log`). Their texts are in Z1 and `RHCommandDeckPanels.cpp`. A greyed SIGN's tooltip equals the dim reason line under the row, which the captures show.
- Balance numbers (the relation gate, the costs, the cargo cap, the 25% for 30 sols, the 6-sol cut, the 9,000 bp ceiling) are chosen for legible arithmetic, not tuned play: awaiting the director.
- The new wording is placeholder (`docs/qa/2026-10-15/gate-d-wording.md`, Z1-Z11 under "Rows added by E6"); "pact" is itself a placeholder. None of it is morale, sickness or evacuation wording, nothing in E6 reads or writes Hope, the population or growth (LV10), and nothing is combat.
- Not committed: the orchestrator commits.

### Idle robots: the design and the director's answers (2026-10-29)

The design is in `docs/qa/2026-10-28/robot-idle-design.md` (DESIGN, written at `4e9a360` while D7 builds; display only under the 2026-10-28 answer (a)). An idle surface walker waits around the pad the sim would dock it at (`FindNearestChargePad` from its sim position); with no working pad it keeps the Lander rank. Now and then it inspects a building within 20 m of the pad, playing Operate for 6 s, chosen by robot index and trip count. Underground robots and rovers are unchanged. Nothing under `Source/RedHopeSim` or `docs/data` changes.

- **Q-A (a):** a loose ring well clear of the pad, about 2.5 m out from its edge, facing outward (8 spots, then 12). The options not taken were (b) a row on one side of the pad and (c) the pad's edge, both of which read more like a charging queue.
- **Q-B (a):** a waiting robot inspects after 20 s of waiting (placeholder): about half its idle time, about 1 robot out at once in the playtest colony. Not taken: (b) 60 s, (c) 3 min.

Not built yet: after E8, from a brief and a gate script.

### T1 (the colony record): the director's answer (2026-10-28)

From `docs/qa/2026-10-28/t1-colony-design.md` (its DIRECTOR QUESTION; review in `t1-review-r1.md`): **(a) one gate, no save change.** T1 moves each colony's state into its own record (`FRHColony`) so a second settlement can exist later; nothing changes on screen. The save format and version stay as they are, so current saves and both checkpoints keep loading. The plan's "list of one colony" in the save moves to T4. Every battery line stays identical apart from wall-clock timing, plus a new `colony.log`, and every captured save stays byte-identical. Not built yet: T1 follows E8 and the idle robots, after a refresh of the design at E8's HEAD.

### D6e lift and tram power: what it settles, and where it deviated (2026-10-28, not yet committed)

Built from `docs/qa/2026-10-26/preflight/D6e-brief.md` on E6's source (`7adc7ee`, save v57). The tree sits on `01f5299`, the docs-only T1 commit made while D6e was being built (`git diff 7adc7ee 01f5299 -- Source docs/data Content scripts` is empty), so D6e commits on `01f5299`. The order of authority, in the queue's order: the DIRECTOR ANSWERs (`docs/qa/2026-10-22/d6-caverns-design.md` Q-A (a), Q-B (a), **Q-C (a): trams carry people only** - no robot tram edge, hold, ride or reserve term; `docs/qa/2026-10-23/d8-deep-view-design.md` **Q-C (a) with R7**: the served-capacity bar ships inside D6e) > `ORCHESTRATOR-RULINGS.md` (1, 3, 6, 7, 8, 9) > `CROSS-CHECK.md` section 6 (items 1, 14, 15) > the brief > the REFRESH AT 31ffa6f > MERGED section 5. Superseded and not built: the draft's section 7 (40 W, 2 W, priority 8, `-liftpower`) and "Save v51" with `OutError` refusals.

**What D6e builds.** (The three rows are placeholders awaiting the director.)
- **Rows** (`docs/data/RH_Config.csv` after `TramPlacesPerSol`, `DT_Config` synced from the whole CSV with `rh_sync_datatables.py`, 316 -> 319 rows): `LiftCarW` 200 W a fitted car, `TramWPerCell` 5 W a chain point, `TransitLoadPriority` 6 (unclamped, like `CoolingLoadPriority`; the draws clamped at 0). Header defaults equal the CSV.
- **Load entries.** `StepPower`'s `FLoadEntry` gains `uint8 Kind` (0 building, 1 cooling, 2 lift, 3 tram) and `int32 Index`. Only with `bHasTransit` (the first car), after D5's cooling entries: one entry per fitted shaft in Id order (`Cars x LiftCarW`), then one per tram line in Id order (`GetTunnelChainLength(StopA, StopB) x TramWPerCell`), counted in `TransitEntries`. With no car nothing is appended and the step is D5's code.
- **RC 7.** Both gates read `(CoolingEntries + TransitEntries) > 0` (the scraping test and the walk). The walk's order is priority, then buildings, cooling, lifts, trams among equals, then the deeper floor among cooling, the lower shaft Id among lifts and the lower line Id among trams. It is built in two parts (see the deviation "the lift and tram entries are merged, not sorted"): the buildings and the cooling entries are sorted alone, by D5's comparator, as the same array D5 sorted; the lift and tram entries, sorted by Kind and Id, are then inserted after the last entry of equal or lower priority. With no transit entry it is D5's sort exactly. In the walk `ShedFloors` counts Kind 1 only, `ShortMask` reads Kind 1 only, and a lift or tram entry in the walked prefix calls `NoteTransitShortMs`. `StepDepthWarmth`'s third argument (`CoolingEntries > 0`), `CoolingW` and `CoolingFloors` are unchanged.
- **The rings (option (a)).** `FRHTransitLedger` and `FRHTramLine` gain runtime `ShortSol[2] = {-1,-1}` and `ShortMs[2] = {0,0}`; `WriteTransitBlock` and `WriteTramBlock` serialize named fields, so their bytes do not move (TR18's and TM6's layout lines are unchanged in the battery). `NoteTransitShortMs` (in `RHSimTransit.cpp`, reusing `TransitSolOf`) tags the slot `sol % 2` with the sol of `GetStepEndSeconds()`, resets an older slot, adds `RoundToInt64(SubDt x 1000)` clamped at 1,200,000, and drops a write into a slot holding a later sol with a Warning (as `ChargeTransit`).
- **RC 8.** `Served = 1000 - min(1000, ShortMs(s-1) / 1200)` permille, read in `RollTransit(SolNow)` for `SolNow - 1` (complete: `StepTransit` runs before `StepPower` in both spines). Only the two capacity arguments of the roll change: a fitted shaft's wait uses `Hoist + (Cars x LiftPlacesPerSol - Hoist) x Served / 1000` (int64), a line's `max(1, TramPlacesPerSol x Served / 1000)`. `GetShaftCapacityPlaces`, `ComputeTransitWaitSec`, TR4's numbers and the robots' reserve are unchanged. Public const accessors: `GetShaftServedPermille`, `GetShaftServedCapacityPlaces` (D8's bar), `GetTramServedPermille`, `GetTramServedCapacityPlaces`, `GetLiftCarW`, `GetTramWPerCell`, `GetTransitLoadPriority` (the getters read `TransitCheckedSol - 1`); harness `Debug_GetTransitShortMs` and `Debug_SetTransitPowerRows` (TP1 (d) only). There is no `Debug_SetTransitPowerScale` and no switch that turns transit power off: `-transit` holds by its bank pin.
- **Save v58, frame `'RHD6'`** (`RHSaveFrame::TransitPowerTag`), written straight after E6's `'RHL6'` and read with `RHSaveFrame::Read` straight after E6's reader (so it consumes the frame): `int32 Count`, then per fitted shaft by Id and per line by Id `Kind, Id, Tag0, Ms0, Tag1, Ms1` (24 bytes; ms clamped again at write). The default colony writes Count 0: a 4-byte payload, 24 bytes of frame. Every repair is a Warning (`LoadColony v58: ...`), never an Error or a refusal: a lost frame and a count outside `0..MaxShaftsCap + MaxTramLinesCap` or not fitting the payload leave every ring empty; an unknown shaft, an unfitted shaft, an unknown line, a repeated Id or a Kind other than 2/3 drops that entry; a slot tagged below -1, of the wrong parity, past `TransitCheckedSol + 1`, or holding ms outside 0..1,200,000 is emptied. A repair costs at most one sol's served share.
- **The literal-version tests** (`git grep -n -P 'Ver == 57\b|"/57"|\(expect 57\)' Source`): 35 literals in 12 files moved to 58 (16 `Ver == 57`, 16 `"/57"`, 3 `(expect 57)`: the brief's 14 `Ver ==` / `/57` sites plus E5's CU10 and E6's LV9 pairs and the commandlet's `/57` and `(expect 57)` texts). No suite asserted that its frame ends the file (E5's CU10, XE10, E6's LV9 and MX1 all check "follows"), so none needed amending.
- **The served-capacity bar (D8 R7, the only UI change).** `RHSectionVisualizerSubsystem.cpp`: the lift label and the bar's `Cap` read `GetShaftServedCapacityPlaces`, the label drops ` (nominal)` (`Shaft %d · %s / %s places · wait %d s · %s commuters a sol`), the legend's long text drops `(nominal)` (the brief's UNCERTAIN default: simplest is to drop it), and the R7 comment is updated. The bar's hash already mixed `Cap`. Gate-D rows M6 and M8 carry the new strings; M6's "`nominal` goes when D6e's served capacity is shown" is resolved.
- **`-transitpower`** (`RHSimTransitPowerSuite.cpp`, `RHRunTransitPowerSuite`, dispatched after `-levers`; `FParse::Param` matches whole switches, so `-transit` never fires for it): TP0 the rows; TP1 D5 DP5's numbers with no car, the draws (+200, +800, +70, +150 W), the shed order at priority 6 and forced to 7, a shed lift not a floor; TP2 a Lander-only sol from an empty bank in both bands (1,200,000 ms, Served 0, capacity 200, 400 places wait 120 s against 0 s nominal) and the same on D5 DP6's floor; TP3 a rule-(ii) step (nothing shed, LoadW 220, +60,000 / +100 ms); TP4 era parity (three cars, six arrays: Served 612 agent / 550 era every sol, worst 62 <= 100; the bank full by day in every sol; onsets 38.2 s apart); TP1 (f) four tied WaterPlants keep D5's order when a car at 0 W is fitted; TP5 mid-sol save twins in both bands, the layout ('RHD6' straight after 'RHL6''s footer, found by tag; Count 0 / 4 bytes by default), the previous version refused (`/58`), thirteen doctored copies each one Warning and no Error (the emptied slots of the tag -2 and wrong-parity cases read back raw from a re-save as tag -1, 0 ms; the repeated-Id case gives the repeat different ms, so the first entry is shown kept); TP6 pinned capacity 1,000 / 4,000 and TR4's 60 s; TP7 a dark tram waits 600 s, not 0. TP4 reads the sim's own `GetShaftServedPermille` right after each roll. 39 checks. `rh_battery.sh` gains it (60 suites, 61 runs; header and echo fixed).

**UNCERTAIN items of the brief, each with its stated default taken.**
- The frame's payload layout (brief 2.6): the brief's own layout, `Count` then 24-byte entries, lifts before trams.
- The legend wording (2.7): `(nominal)` dropped, nothing added.
- DC9's delta (cross-check item 14 settled it): +24, observed.
- The ring storage (2.2): option (a), checked first (the transit and tram blocks write named fields).
- The lifts=1 playtest at 200 W (section 3, a charge-0 or no-delivery failure): did not happen - no stop, 0 failures in both the early and the final run; its other numbers move (below).

**Deviations.**
- MERGED section 10's item 6: **lift power cuts capacity, not ride speed**, and a lift's or line's own shortfall has no rating, arrivals or stranding effect: a dark sol raises only the next sol's waits (and, through them, the commute, `CommuteWaitCm`). **The draw itself is an ordinary load on the shared bank**: 200 W a car empties a scraping bank sooner, like any machine, so other loads shed sooner or more often, and those include life support (the AirFilter, priority 8) and the floor ratings that hang on it. That is an indirect effect of any load and is not special to lifts; the lifts=1 playtest below shows it at the placeholder 200 W. The rows are the director's to settle.
- **The D6e block is a framed tail block that repairs** with Warnings (ruling 3, option (i)), never a mid-file block that refuses.
- **SP1 adopted:** a line's capacity is floored at 1 place, so a dark sol reads a crowd as `MaxWaitSec` (TP7), never 0 (`ComputeTransitWaitSec` returns 0 for a capacity of 0).
- **SP2 adopted:** a shed lift or line counts in `ShedCount` only, never in `ShedFloors`; the POWER tile (`RHCommandDeckPanels.cpp`, `ShedCount - ShedFloors`) therefore calls a shed lift a "machine" (placeholder copy, unchanged).
- **SP3 adopted:** ms clamped at 1,200,000 when added and again at write, so a legitimate file never meets the "ms above one sol" repair.
- **The lift and tram entries are merged, not sorted** (the brief has one sort of every entry with the Kind comparator; the first build did that). `TArray::Sort` is an unstable introsort and two buildings of equal priority compare equal, so the order it leaves them in depends on the whole array: sorting the longer array reordered tied buildings - which of two AirFilters sheds first - with or without any draw. The first build's `-scale` showed it (below: two floors lost their rating and 12 colonists were recalled, at 0 W as at 200 W). The buildings and cooling are now sorted alone as D5 sorted them, and the transit entries are inserted at their priority; TP1 (f) pins it, and mutation 7 (the first build's single sort) turns TP1 (f) red.
- **Repairs beyond the brief's list**, each a Warning and each exercised in TP5 (e): a slot tagged past `TransitCheckedSol + 1` is emptied (no spine can stamp one: `StepTransit` rolls before `StepPower`; without it a doctored future tag would log the late-write Warning at every step), and a count whose bytes do not exactly fill the payload (too few or too many) empties every ring. TP5 (e) runs the brief's eight doctored copies plus five (a future tag, a repeated Id, a bad Kind, a count of -1, a count of `MaxShaftsCap + MaxTramLinesCap + 1`).
- **The count bound uses the compile-time caps**, `RH::MaxShaftsCap + RH::MaxTramLinesCap` (16 + 32 = 48), not the brief's `MaxShafts + MaxTramLinesCap`: a colony whose `MaxShafts` row was lowered after it sank its shafts keeps its rings. The exact-size test still binds, and an entry naming a shaft the colony lacks is dropped with its Warning.
- **TP1 (c)'s fixture includes the Pylon** that shaft 1 is sunk beside (priority 1, 10 W), so its full load is 680 W, not the 670 W the brief's list implies; the order it checks is the brief's.
- **A lost 'RHD6' frame always warns** (no ruling-2 "only when it held something to lose" rule was needed: no existing log loses the frame; the battery shows no `LoadColony v58` line outside `transitpower.log`).
- **SP5, a follow-up, not built:** the shaft card (`RHCommandDeck.cpp`, "load N / 4000") and `RH.Transit` (`RHCheats.cpp`) still show the nominal capacity.
- The new wording is placeholder: rows M6 and M8, and the developer-facing block A1-A7 in `docs/qa/2026-10-15/gate-d-wording.md` (prefix A, the builder's choice: free at HEAD per CROSS-CHECK section 5, `git grep -n -P '^\| ?A[0-9]+ '` found no row, and no brief claims it; ruling 6 assigns D6e no prefix; the orchestrator confirmed A as ruling 17). Nothing in D6e reads or writes Hope, the population, growth or Influence directly (the draw can move them only through the shared bank; see MERGED item 6), and nothing is combat. The orchestrator accepted the sort merge and the `MaxShaftsCap` bound as rulings 15 and 16 (`ORCHESTRATOR-RULINGS.md`, 2026-10-29).

**Moved lines in the battery** (diff against `Saved/baselines/battery_post_e6`, 74 differing lines = 37 pairs, 61 logs): the 31 save-version lines in 17 logs (the 27 of d842dd0 plus CU10 x2 and LV9 x2; the `LoadColony v55/v56/v57:` literals, the Hope `expect 55.00` and the dayshift `expect 5520` lines did not move), of which CV8 and DP11 also carry the whole-file sizes; the unmasked size lines each +24 (CV8 12370 -> 12394 and 12450 -> 12474, DP11 12398 -> 12422 and 12430 -> 12454, EC1 era and agent 13411 -> 13435, DC9 13719 -> 13743); three wall-clock lines (economy EC7a, eraparity EP4, traffic X16; arrivals e did not move this run); and the new `transitpower.log`. Everything else is line-identical.

**Moved outputs outside the battery.**
- **`-scale`** (run once on each binary; 195 `SCALE ok`, 0 FAIL, 0 errors on both). Final binary against the pre-change one: one non-timing line, the depth case's `StepPower with 15 cooling entries and a scraping bank sheds 978` -> `982` (the 16 cars' four lift entries, all in the shed prefix of an empty-bank walk). Every digest is unchanged. Timing: the depth case's StepPower 0.04049 -> 0.04106 ms (budget 0.05); the deep commute's cold commute 9.229 -> 8.962 ms; the rest within noise.
- **The first build's `-scale` moved more, and the cause was a defect, now fixed.** With every entry in one sort, the deep-commute setup gained `=== FLOOR -16 LOST HABITABILITY (83856 / 84000 kg O2) ===` and the same for -17, and 12 `=== COLONIST EVACUATED: ... (Floor -16/-17, rations short 0.1/0.2 sols) ===` lines (two batches of six); it lost `=== HAB NETWORK #148 LOST HABITABILITY (50242 / 50400 kg O2) ===` and `=== HOME RATIONS RESTORED: Floor -17 (133 residents) ===` / `Floor -16`; one `MOMENT dispute` line changed; and the deep-commute and trams cases measured 4787 colonists, not 4799 (4434 long, not 4446). The mechanism, traced with a temporary log of the shed walk (removed): the depth case's 1,000 `Debug_StepPower` calls on an empty bank (GenW 50 W against about 105 kW) shed 982 of 987 entries and leave the last call's `bPowered` flags in place; the colony is saved and loaded with them, and the deep commute's first era step reads circulation (`IsFloorCirculated`) from them, so a floor whose AirFilter that last walk left unpowered loses its rating (`!bCirculated`; the fill, 83856 of 84000 kg, is not the cause) and its residents go on short air rations. The five entries that walk leaves powered are the last in its order, three of them priority-8 AirFilters, and which three depends on the unstable sort's order among tied buildings: D5's order (the merged build's log; the pre-change binary's lines agree) left #964 (-16), #965 (-17) and #6 (-3) powered and shed #150 (habitat #148's), #7 (-4) and #8 (-5); the one-array sort, given four more entries, shed #964, #965 and #6 and left the other three powered. The same lines moved at `LiftCarW = 0, TramWPerCell = 0` (so not the draw) and went back with the merged sort at 200 W and at 0 W (so not the rings). Not intended: fixed (the merge), pinned by TP1 (f) and mutation 7.
- **The default playtest** (`d6e_default`, districts off) is byte-identical to `d6e_default_pre`, and `d6e_full` to `Saved/baselines/ind_full_playtest.txt`.
- **The lifts=1 playtest at 110 sols** (`RH_PLAYTEST_DISTRICTS=1 RH_PLAYTEST_LIFTS=1`), outside the byte-identical set. The brief's UNCERTAIN (a charge-0 or no-delivery failure at 200 W) did not happen: every `lifts:` check passes, 0 failures. But the run moves. `d6e_lifts_pre` (no draw): `rides 12, hand-off holds 3 | deliveries below 5, builds below 1 | most robots below at once 1 | charge-0 ticks below 0`; power short 40% of the run, machines shed 29%, the bank empty 1365 of 2640 sol-hours; 179 beds, population 110. `d6e_lifts_final` (200 W, reproduced byte for byte by a second run): `rides 10, hand-off holds 2 | deliveries below 4, builds below 1 | most robots below at once 2 | charge-0 ticks below 0`; power short 50%, machines shed 15%, the bank empty 764 sol-hours; 166 beds, population 118. On the final binary the same run at `LiftCarW = 0, TramWPerCell = 0` is byte-identical to `d6e_lifts_pre`, so the whole shift is the 200 W draw. Information for the director's review of the placeholder rows; no stop (ruling 9).
- **`RH.DeepBench 3000`** (1440x860, `RH.FrameStats 15`, the section focus -10 for window A, then TRAFFIC for B), median / p95 ms: pre-change A 25.24 / 25.85 and 26.55 / 27.11, B 25.01 / 25.51 and 25.69 / 26.13 (two runs); final A 26.79 / 28.10 and 26.55 / 27.20, B 26.03 / 26.76 and 26.03 / 26.76 (two runs; `Saved/RHCapture/d6e/bench/bench_final_r1.log`, `_r2.log`). The medians stay within about 1 ms of the pre-change runs (window A's second runs match at 26.55), which is the bench's run-to-run spread on this machine; nothing in D6e's presentation change (one label and one bar denominator) is expected to cost a frame. The bench pins its bank only at setup and its city is power-starved, so its 12 cars (2.4 kW) are short most of each sol: its lift labels read 200 places with waits of tens of seconds by sol 2 at 60x.

**Gate runs outside the logs above.** Click test at 1440x860 (`rh_clicktest.sh <label> 450`, the timeout raised through the second argument): six runs on the final binary; runs 1, 2, 4, 5 and 6 PASS 94 of 94, run 3 FAILED one step, `leak.total` ("6 world click(s) reached OnClick, 5 made on purpose"), the intermittent failure an earlier gate's log also shows (`Saved/RHCapture/logs/sites_r3_click4.log`, the same text); runs 4-6 are the three back to back. Step texts are E6's except the measured label band of `section.labels`, `.traffic` and `.turned` (`free band 351..1401` -> `126..1176`, `ladder at 514` -> `289`), the same 225 px shift in every run of this session and in the deep-bench scene (`415..1401` -> `190..1176`); the first build's runs the evening before read E6's values, and D6e's only presentation change is one label's text, so this is taken to be the session's window, not D6e - stated, not proven. `tram.setup` reads as before. The deep-bench scene passes 9 of 9. Both checkpoints were re-cut at v58 (Midgame 15821 bytes, Late 18013, header version 0x3a) and load in one boot with no Warning.
